- Answer from this content only. If it is not covered, say so; never guess numbers.
- Find relevant pages in index.md by title, aliases (aka) and categories.
- [[X]] links to page X; [[X|y]] and 'y ([[X]])' are text y linking to X. Follow links and each page's 'Linked from' list to gather related facts (e.g. where an item drops or is used).
- Cite the [[Page]] each fact comes from. Reply in the user's language.

# Librarian
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Librarian]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Library Card ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various [[Enchanting]]-related items.
Location: [[Library]]
Coordinates: -68.5 70 -79.5
The Librarian is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Library]]. They sell a variety of [[Enchanting]]-related items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Librarian: Greetings! Welcome to the Library! [NPC] Librarian: The Library is your one-stop shop for all things enchanting. Enchant items, purchase Enchanted Books, and more! [NPC] Librarian: You can enchant items by clicking any Enchantment Table. Enchanting costs experience levels - the more levels you spend, the better enchantments you will receive. [NPC] Librarian: Use the Enchantment Table to enchant an item!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Librarian: Greetings! I've been running low on paper recently for my Enchanted Books, could you bring me some Sugar Cane?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Librarian: I'll be sure to make some Enchanted Books out of these, I hope you come around some day!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Librarian: Very unfortunate, I really needed that paper...|
## Garden Visitor

# Liz
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Liz]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells items in exchange for [[Stock of Stonks]] while [[Diaz]] is in office.
Location: [[Trade Center]]
Coordinates: -35.5 70 -77.5
Liz is an [[NPC]] in the [[Trade Center]] that sells various items in exchange for [[Stock of Stonks]] while [[Diaz]] is in office with the Stock Exchange perk.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|When Diaz is not in office|[NPC] Liz: Welcome to the Trade Center! [NPC] Liz: Sorry, but there's... not much to see here right now. [NPC] Liz: We're friends of Diaz's, so we only really have stuff to do when she's elected as Mayor, haha. [NPC] Liz: Try dropping by when she's in office!|
|First Interaction when Diaz is in office|[NPC] Liz: Richard thinks he's it, being the Stonks Auction Manager. [NPC] Liz: Jerk. [NPC] Liz: If not for me and my shop, people wouldn't even CARE about Stock of Stonks. [NPC] Liz: Yup, that's right - you can trade me Stock of Stonks for some special items. [NPC] Liz: Most of them are Crafting materials used to upgrade various Coin-related pieces of gear. [NPC] Liz: Better hurry, though because they're only available during Mayor Diaz's term. [NPC] Liz: Here's what I've got.|
## Trivia
- Excluding [[Golden Bounty]], utilizing all of Liz's products to max potential requires 338 (5 stacks plus 18) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]].
  - Total of 116 (1 stack plus 52) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]] are required to get [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] for both [[Relic of Coins]] and [[Scavenger Artifact]].
  - Additional 72 (1 stack plus 8) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]] are required to upgrade all items using [[Golden Fragment|Golden Fragments]].
  - And 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]] are required for both [[Crown of Avarice]] and [[Emerald Artifact]].
- According to the person who came up with the idea of [[Trade Center]] and [[Diaz]]'s "Long Term Investment" perk, Liz is [[Diaz]]'s sister.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 3, 2024|Added Liz.|
|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Liz from -55.5, 72, -56.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to -35.5, 70, -77.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2024|0.20.3|Added Liz.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Liz from -55.5, 72, -56.5 ([[Coordinates]]) to -35.5, 70, -77.5 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Lone Adventurer
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Lone Adventurer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Talk to Lone Adventurer ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Sells the [[Void Sword]], and tasks the player with killing 5 Endermen ([[Enderman]]).
Location: [[The End]]
Coordinates: -524.5 101 -275.5
Description: Tasks the player with obtaining different pieces of [[Ender Armor]].
Location: [[The End]]
Coordinates: -588.5 22 -270.5
The Lone Adventurer is an [[NPC]] located in [[The End]].
## Quest
Upon first speaking to the Lone Adventurer, they ask the player to kill 5 [[Enderman|Endermen]]. After doing so, the Lone Adventurer will offer the player a [[Void Sword]], for 200,000 [[Coins]]. After either purchasing or not purchasing it, the Lone Adventurer will then move to the [[Dragon's Nest]], and ask the player to obtain all 8 pieces of [[Ender Armor]]. Once wearing the full set of Ender Armor, the Lone Adventurer will ask the player to kill [[Seer|Seers]] to obtain a [[Dragon Shortbow]]. Once having obtained it, the [[Player]] must bring it back to the Lone Adventurer. Then, The Lone Adventurer asks the player to reach Combat Skill 18. Once reached, the player will then have to wait 1 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock day]] before talking to them again. Once the time has passed, the Lone Adventurer will offer a menu displaying the [[Player]]'s [[Ender Dragon|Dragon]] stats.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Exhausted? me? No no no. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: I'm just taking a break. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: The End is a creepy place, but you get used to it! [NPC] Lone Adventurer: If you want a piece of advice, you should start by killing the Endermen up here. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: They sometimes drop important gear like the armor I'm wearing. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: Are you strong enough though? [NPC] Lone Adventurer: Try killing 5 of them!|
|After Killing 5 Endermen|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Alright, not bad, not bad! [NPC] Lone Adventurer: It took me a while to get that strong. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: I use a Void Sword, it's a very powerful weapon. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: It gets stronger with each piece of Ender Armor you are wearing. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: I have an extremely strong emotional attachment to this item so... [NPC] Lone Adventurer: I'm willing to sell it to you for the modest sum of 200,000 coins. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: What do you say? Select an option: [YES] [NO]|
|Buying [[Void Sword]]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Happy dealing with you, I hope this sword will help you as much as it helped me.|
|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: I think you are making a mistake, perhaps the biggest mistake of your life! [NPC] Lone Adventurer: Are you sure you don't want that sword? Select an option: [I am sure] [Fine I'll buy your sword]|
|Selecting [I am sure]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Strange. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: If you change your mind you can also go talk to Rusty in the Gold Mine. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: Distracted people sometimes lose their Void Sword and he sells them back.|
|After Buying or Not Buying [[Void Sword]]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: You should focus on getting all 8 pieces of the Ender Armor now. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: Half of them are dropped by the Endermen on this layer, the other pieces are a little harder to obtain. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: I'm sure you'll figure it out. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: My time here is over, I think I'll go down in the Dragon's Nest very soon. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: See you around!|

|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Ah, do you need help finding the remaining pieces? Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Which piece are you struggling to find? Select an option: [Cloak] [Belt] [Necklace] [Gauntlet] [Armor]|
|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Okay. If you need help, I'll be here.|
|Selecting [Cloak]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: This one isn't too bad. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: You see those Seers inside the Dragon's Nest? [NPC] Lone Adventurer: If you take down enough of those you'll end up getting the Cloak.|
|Selecting [Belt]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: The Obsidian Defenders drop those Belts rarely, at least that's how I got mine.|
|Selecting [Necklace]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: You haven't got this one yet? [NPC] Lone Adventurer: It's just from regular Endermen, like the rest of the armor!|
|Selecting [Gauntlet]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Ahh, this one is a bit tricky. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: You see those purple End Stone or Obsidian that sometimes appear around here? [NPC] Lone Adventurer: If you mine enough Ender Nodes you'll eventually find the Gauntlet.|
|Selecting [Armor]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Really? [NPC] Lone Adventurer: It's from those Endermen up there, where we first met.|

|Wearing [[Ender Armor]] and [[Equipment]]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Woah, you're wearing the full Ender Set! [NPC] Lone Adventurer: That's incredible, that took me at least a week!|

|Interacting "later"|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Greetings <player>! [NPC] Lone Adventurer: For the longest time I thought I would move to the Crimson Isle or visit the Catacombs after this place. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: You already made a ton of progress and I'm excited for the future. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: ⚑ Dragon Guide is my new vocation. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: Ask me anything regarding Dragons!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|

|Before Finding [[Ender Armor]]; Before Lone Adventurer moves to the Dragon's Nest|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Someone called me an "Ender noob" the other day, that was pretty rude.|
|(same as above)|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: You should look for End Stone or Obsidian with purple glows. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: They contain cool loot when mined!|
|Before Finding [[Dragon Shortbow]]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: Please bring me back my bow if you find it. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: I'm fairly sure one of those Seers has it.|
|Before Talking to [[Pearl Dealer]]|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: The Dragon Shortbow is a powerful weapon. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: I'm going to use it to kill more Dragons as soon as I'm fully recovered.|
|Before Reaching Combat level 18|[NPC] Lone Adventurer: I'd tell you more about Dragons but you're not even Combat level 18. [NPC] Lone Adventurer: Try your luck slaying some Zealots.|
## Trivia
- Once the Lone Adventurer's [[Quests|Quest]] has been completed, Dragon Specialist will be displayed over their head.
- The Lone Adventurer begins with [[Ender Armor]], before moving on to [[Young Dragon Armor]] and finally [[Superior Dragon Armor]].
  - Whilst in [[Young Dragon Armor]] the Lone Adventurer can be seen with their [Lv46] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Enderman Pet]]. They will change to having an [[Ender Dragon Pet]] when in [[Superior Dragon Armor]].
- Despite their name, the Lone Adventurer seeks help from the player.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Oct 19, 2022|0.16|Added Lone Adventurer.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed being unable to proceed past the [[Dragon Shortbow]] questline if the item was collected previously.|

Lonely Philosopher is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Ruins]]. They sell a [[Travel Scroll to Hub Castle]] to players with 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] or above.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Without 5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]])|[NPC] Lonely Philosopher: I'm sorry, I have nothing for you.|
|5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]]) or Above|[NPC] Lonely Philosopher: To fast travel or not to fast travel?|
## Trivia
- The Lonely Philosopher's 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] quote is a reference to a quote from Hamlet.

# Lucius
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Lucius]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the [[Shady Ring]] and its upgrades after purchasing certain amounts of items from the [[Dark Auction]].
Location: [[Wilderness]]
Coordinates: 125 73 165
Lucius is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wilderness]]. They sell the [[Shady Ring]] and its upgrades to the player.
## Shop
Lucius will sell a line of [[Accessories]] to the player after they have purchased certain amounts of items from the [[Dark Auction]]. These Accessories give discounts at most NPC shops. Each Accessory can only be purchased once per player profile.
|Item|Requirement|Cost|
|-|-|-|
|[[Shady Ring]]|1 item purchased from [[Dark Auction]].|500,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Crooked Artifact]]|3 items purchased from [[Dark Auction]].|2,000,000 [[Coins]] [[Shady Ring]]|
|[[Seal of the Family]]|7 items purchased from [[Dark Auction]].|10,000,000 [[Coins]] [[Crooked Artifact]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before purchasing an item from [[Dark Auction]]|[NPC] Lucius: Psst! Listen here! [NPC] Lucius: Not too loud! [NPC] Lucius: My cousin Sirius leads an... organization. [NPC] Lucius: It's a... business, auctioning sweet items. [NPC] Lucius: He regularly runs events at a nearby house, but only under the cover of night. [NPC] Lucius: Buy a few items from Sirius and come back!|
|Purchasing less than 3 items from the [[Dark Auction]]|[NPC] Lucius: You've purchased <number> items from the Dark Auction. [NPC] Lucius: Come back when you've purchased 3. I'll have something for you then.|
|Purchasing less than 7 items from the [[Dark Auction]]|[NPC] Lucius: You've purchased <number> items from the Dark Auction. [NPC] Lucius: Come back when you've purchased 7. I'll have something for you then.|
|After purchasing 7; items from [[Dark Auction]]|[NPC] Lucius: You've purchased 7 items from the Dark Auction. [NPC] Lucius: You're basically family at this point.|
## Trivia
- Purchasing the [[Seal of the Family]] from Lucius awards the [[Player]] with the [[Achievements|"Lifelong Contract" Achievement]].
- The name Lucius is derived from the Latin lūx, which means light, and is a cognate both with Lucifer ("light-bringer" in Latin) and the name Lucas (It is derived from the Latin verb "lucere", meaning "to shine".).
- Lucius' name is a reference to the Harry Potter books. In Harry Potter, Lucius Malfoy is Sirius Black's cousin-in-law.
  - The Latin name Sirius has roots in Ancient Greek, who's theorised meanings include "sparkling" "flickering" or "scorching", drawing another parallel between the two NPCs.
- Hypixel started counting the number of items each player purchases from the Dark Auction on Dec 4th, 2019. All items purchased prior to that date do not count as bought items.
## History

|Jan 30, 2026|Moved Lucius from 125, 73, 165 ([[Coordinates]]) to 125, 73, 165 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Lucius.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Lucius from 125, 73, 165 ([[Coordinates]]) to 125, 73, 165 ([[Coordinates]]), due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Ludleth
Aliases: Ludeth
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Ludleth]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells about [[Rose Dragon Pet]] after the player has completed their offer on [[The Garden]].
Location: [[Farm]]
Coordinates: 81.5 59 -112.5
Ludleth is an [[NPC]] that only appears in [[The Garden]]. They can appear after the player has reached [[The Garden|Garden XV (15)]] and served 100 unique [[The Garden|Visitors]]. Completing their offer allows Ludleth to move to a cave surrounded by rose bushes in [[Hub Island|The Hub]] and sell the [[Rose Dragon Pet]] for 500,000,000 [[Coins]], 20,000 [[Copper]], 5 [[Condensed Helianthus]], 1 [[All-in Aloe]], 1 [[Glasscorn]], 1 [[Devourer]], 1 [[Phantomleaf]], and 1 [[Timestalk]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Garden visit Request|[NPC] Ludleth: You've amassed quite a fortune. I have an antiquity that might interest you. However, I wouldn't sell it to just anybody, you know? I need something in return. So, what do you say?|
|First Garden visit Completing|[NPC] Ludleth: I'm glad we are in agreement, <Player>. Meet me in the Hub, we'll discuss our business then.|
|First Garden visit Denying|[NPC] Ludleth: Not everyone can afford my lifestyle!|
|Subsequent Garden visit Request|[NPC] Ludleth: Pleasure seeing you again, <Player>!|
|The Hub First interaction|[NPC] Ludleth: Pleasure seeing you again, <Player>. I have a fine offer for you...|
|The Hub After Purchasing|[NPC] Ludleth: Every rose has its...claws!|
## Trivia
- Ludleth is the only [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] visitor to not request unique items.

# Lumina
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines
[Lumina]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells items that can light up areas.
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 77.5 199 -110.5
Lumina is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. They sell various items with the intent of lighting up areas.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Lumina: Looking to protect yourself or light up some tunnels? [NPC] Lumina: We've got torches, lanterns. Pretty much anything to suit your needs.|
|Purchasing an Item|[NPC] Lumina: Good doin' business with ya.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Lumina: I'm looking to make some Jack o' Lanterns to help light up the Crystal Hollows - maybe you can help me out?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Lumina: Nice doin' business with ya! Here's some copper in return.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Lumina: Not a problem, thanks for your time anyways.|
## Trivia
- The name "Lumina" is probably a reference to the fact that she sells items that can illuminate areas.
- Some dialogue lines are references to merchant dialogue lines in Skyrim. The original lines Lumina's dialogue is based off of is displayed below.
  Looking to protect yourself, or deal some damage?
  Hmm... Blades, helmets. Pretty much anything to suit your needs.
  Good doin' business with ya.

# Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters
[Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Gives the player a [[Combat]]-related quest outside the Rift.
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: 23.5 154.12 43
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X is an [[NPC]] on [[The Mountaintop]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They give the player a [[Combat]]-related quest outside the Rift.
## Purpose
Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X gives the player a [[Combat]] quest that they must complete outside the Rift.
Behind the nearby mirror, a replica of the player is shown fighting with a [[Raider Sword|Raider Axe]] in [[Arachne's Burrow]] while equipped with [[Strong Dragon Armor]] and a [Lv1] [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Wolf Pet]].
To complete the quest, all of the following conditions must be met at the same time:
- Wearing a full Armor set of [[Strong Dragon Armor]], [[Rampart Armor]], [[Perfect Armor]] (Tier 12), [[Berserker Armor]], [[Rekindled Ember Armor]], [[Frozen Blaze Armor]], [[Young Dragon Armor]], [[Wise Dragon Armor]], [[Protector Dragon Armor]], [[Old Dragon Armor]], [[Superior Dragon Armor]], [[Unstable Dragon Armor]], [[Reaper Armor]], [[Final Destination Armor]], [[Crimson Hunter Set]], [[Aurora Armor]], [[Fervor Armor]], [[Hollow Armor]], [[Terror Armor]], [[Necromancer Lord Armor]], [[Wither Armor]], [[Necron's Armor]], [[Storm's Armor]], [[Goldor's Armor]], [[Maxor's Armor]], [[Crimson Armor]], [[Crimson Armor|Hot Crimson Armor]], [[Crimson Armor|Burning Crimson Armor]], [[Crimson Armor|Fiery Crimson Armor]] or [[Crimson Armor|Infernal Crimson Armor]]
- Equipping one of the following pets of [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] rarity or higher: [[Wolf Pet]], [[Ender Dragon Pet]], [[Golden Dragon Pet]], [[Enderman Pet]], [[Kuudra Pet]], [[Phoenix Pet]] or [[Tiger Pet]]
- Holding any of the following Weapons: [[Enrager]], [[Halberd of the Shredded]], [[Soul Whip]], [[Midas' Sword]], [[Yeti Sword]], [[Silk-Edge Sword]], [[Florid Zombie Sword]], [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], [[Phantom Rod]], [[Pigman Sword]], [[Aspect of the Dragons]], [[Atomsplit Katana]], [[Reaper Scythe]], [[Livid Dagger]], [[Shadow Fury]], [[Flower of Truth]], [[Wither Cloak Sword]], [[Necromancer Sword]], [[Giant's Sword]], [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]], [[Hyperion]], [[Astraea]], [[Scylla]], [[Valkyrie]], [[Dark Claymore]] or [[Raider Sword|Raider Axe]]
- Being within [[Arachne's Burrow]] or [[Arachne's Sanctuary]] in the [[Spider's Den]]
  - Vanishing by leaving the island via a command, such as /hub, or disconnecting
Once completed, the message "You sense a watchful presence..." will appear in chat. The player can then return to Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X to complete the mirror-related puzzle.
After completing all mirror-related puzzles, the player can purchase [[Shen's Ringalia]] for 25,000 [[Motes]], 64 (1 stack) [[Puff Crux]], 20 [[Youngite]], 20 [[Timite]], 20 [[Obsolite]] and 1 [[Disinfestor Gloves]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: I seek a particular warrior. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Reflected in this mirror... could that be you? Select an option: [They definitely look like me.]|
|Selecting [They definitely look like me.]|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Confirm your identity for me. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: From beyond the mirror, wear a similar set of combat gear, or stronger. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Then, I want to see you vanish out of thin air.|
|After completing this quest without completing all other Eleanor quests|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Very well. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: You've proven your identity. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Perhaps you are the one my master seeks. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: But first, you must demonstrate your worth to the other Eleanors. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Until then, I cannot grant you Shen's Ringalia.|
|After completing all mirror-related puzzles|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Very well. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: You've proven your identity. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: It seems you are indeed whom my master seeks. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: I will allow you to purchase Shen's Ringalia.|
|After completing all mirror-related puzzles without accepting the offer|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: It seems you are indeed whom my master seeks. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: I will allow you to purchase Shen's Ringalia.|
|After purchasing [[Shen's Ringalia]]|[NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: You must now seek my master, at the end of the road. [NPC] Madame Eleanor Q. Goldsworth X: Good luck with the rest of your adventures.|

|Jan 21, 2025|0.21.1|Added [[Disinfestor Gloves]] to the cost of [[Shen's Ringalia]].|

# Maddox the Slayer
Aliases: Crappox, Maddox, Maddox The Slayer
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Slayer, Zombie Slayer, Spider Slayer, Wolf Slayer, Enderman Slayer, Blaze Slayer, NPCs with Epithets
[Maddox the Slayer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to start [[Slayer]] quests.
Location: [[Tavern]]
Coordinates: -83.5 68 -129.5
Maddox the Slayer is an [[NPC]] located at the [[Tavern]] basement. The player can participate in a [[Slayer]] quest after speaking to Maddox the Slayer and paying [[Coins]]. The player can access Maddox the Slayer from anywhere by using the [[Maddox Batphone]] once reaching [[Wolf Slayer|Wolf Slayer III (3)]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Maddox: Do you thirst for adventure? [NPC] Maddox: Do you wish to slay the mightiest beasts of the land? [NPC] Maddox: Complete Slayer Quests and fight bosses to unlock exotic rewards!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ Abi...phone? [NPC] Maddox: ✆ Give me my Batphone back. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without [[Maddox Batphone]]|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ You don't have what I need! Nice try!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Maddox Batphone]]|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ I was the first to offer magical over-the-air contact. Don't you forget it.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ Hello?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ You again? What do you want this time?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ Someone answers!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ You hear the line pick up...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ Hey, what do you need?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Maddox: ✆ How does a lobster answer? Shello!|
## Trivia
- Maddox is [[Aatrox]]'s older brother. Aatrox's bonuses when [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] are each intended to improve slayers.
  - In the original patch notes for the 0.9 update, Aatrox is referred to as [[Maddox the Slayer]] Maddox the Slayer's brother-in-law.
- A 5% discount for [[Slayer]] quests was originally planned from the [[City Projects/Bartender's Brewery|Bartender's Brewery]] City Project, but this was changed to be 4% for all players if they reached level 7 on all slayer bosses, and an additional 1% if a player completed all the bonuses for the Bartender's Brewery City Project.
- Maddox and his brother [[Aatrox]] are from both from [[Scarleton]], on the [[Crimson Isle]].
- Maddox is referred to by [[Tal Ker]] as "Crappox",

# Maddox the Slayer (Rift)
Aliases: Maddox The Slayer (Rift)
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Slayer, Vampire Slayer, NPCs with Epithets
[Maddox the Slayer]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to start [[Vampire Slayer]] quests.
Location: [[Stillgore Château]]
Coordinates: 204.5 77 45
Maddox the Slayer is an [[NPC]] located at the [[Stillgore Château]] in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]]. The player can start a [[Slayer]] quest after speaking to Maddox the Slayer and paying [[Motes]]. The player can access Maddox the Slayer from anywhere within the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] by using the [[Maddox Badphone]] once reaching [[Vampire Slayer|Vampire Slayer II (2)]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Before showing a [[Healing Melon]]|[NPC] Maddox the Slayer: I'm not letting magic, dimensions, or the arrest of time stop me from grinding. [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: If there's something to slay, I show up no matter what. [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: Today, vampires are on the menu. [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: Be careful because they deal ❤ damage and if you die around here... [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: ... believe it or not, right to jail. [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: So you need to heal! [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: With melons! [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: Slay a few vampires, then craft and show me your Healing Melon!|
|Showing a [[Healing Melon]]|[NPC] Maddox the Slayer: Very good. [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: Now let's get down to business. [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: I'm not letting magic, inter-dimensional travel, or the arrest of time stop me from grinding. [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: Day, night, tenebrous void... [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: Don't care! [NPC] Maddox the Slayer: I show up anyway!|
## Trivia
- Maddox's dialogue regarding inter-dimensional travel implies both [[Maddox the Slayer (Rift)]] Maddox the Slayer and his hub's counterpart [[Maddox the Slayer]] are the same entity.
  - This is makes [[Maddox the Slayer (Rift)]] Maddox the Slayer one of the few [[NPC]]'s able to traverse between the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] and the [[SkyBlock|Overworld]].
- Maddox's dialogue "... believe it or not, right to jail." is a reference to the iconic "Right to Jail" monologue in Parks and Recreation Season 2 Episode 5 titled Sister City.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Maddox the Slayer.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Maddox the Slayer.|

# Malik
Aliases: Dungeon Blacksmith
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dungeons
[Malik]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Allows the player to upgrade or salvage their items, and grants access to the Wither Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) and Undead Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]).
Location: [[Catacombs Entrance]]
Coordinates: -80.5 56 -119.5
Description: Allows the player to upgrade or salvage their items, and grants access to the Wither Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]) and Undead Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]).
Location: [[Dungeon Hub]]
Coordinates: -49.5 119 10.5
Malik is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] and the [[Dungeon Hub]]. They allow the player to dungeonize and star their gear, grants access to both the [[Essence Shops|Wither]] and [[Essence Shops|Undead Essence Shops]] but not to its [[Salvaging]] menu.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; After 0.7.11|[NPC] Malik: Hello. [NPC] Malik: The Catacombs are an old maze under the island. [NPC] Malik: Tunnels in the caves were created to bury victims of the Calamity. [NPC] Malik: An evil force took control of the graves. [NPC] Malik: Corpses are raised into a powerful army fueled by pain, misery and anguish. [NPC] Malik: Anyway! [NPC] Malik: If you find gear inside, I can upgrade it for you!|
|None; Before 0.7.11|[NPC] Malik: Once my hammer delivery is here, I'll let you use my services!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Malik: ✆ Hello. [NPC] Malik: ✆ I don't tend to give out my contact to those delving into The Catacombs. [NPC] Malik: ✆ I used to, but remembering who's still alive and who isn't is a nightmare. [NPC] Malik: ✆ By the end of any given week, 90% of the people in my directory would be dead. [NPC] Malik: ✆ Removing them was a real pain. [NPC] Malik: ✆ Anyways, I guess I'll add you, but only if you can prove to me that you won't just die like everyone else. [NPC] Malik: ✆ Survive long enough to collect 5,000 Undead Essence, and then bring it to me. [GIVE ESSENCE]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Clicking [GIVE ESSENCE] without 5,000 (78 stacks plus 8) [[Undead Essence]]|[NPC] Malik ✆ You don't have enough Undead Essence. [NPC] Malik ✆ I asked for 5,000 of it. [NPC] Malik ✆ Come back when you've got enough.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Clicking [GIVE ESSENCE] with 5,000 [[Undead Essence]]|[NPC] Malik: ✆ Thank you. [NPC] Malik: ✆ Here's my contact. ✆ Malik has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Malik: ✆ I wish you the best of luck in The Catacombs. [NPC] Malik: ✆ Try to stay alive, for both your sake and mine - I don't feel like expending the minimal effort required to remove you from my contacts.|
## Trivia
- The name Malik originated in West Asia, used in Arabic, Urdu, Persian, Hebrew, Aramaic among other languages. It has multiple latinised spellings with similar meanings, such as: Malik or Malek meaning "owner" in Arabic or Malyk and Malyeek which mean "king".

# Marigold
Aliases: Midas Lure, Midas lure
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs

  Not to be confused with [[Chocolate Rabbits|Marigold]], a Country Faction Rabbit.
Marigold is an [[NPC]] in the [[Royal Mines]] section of the [[Dwarven Mines]]. They have an [[Essence Shops|Essence Shop]] where the player can purchase perks using [[Gold Essence]] after defeating a [[Golden Goblin]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; Haven't killed a Golden Goblin yet|[NPC] Marigold: Hey! I'm Marigold! I don't recall seeing you around these parts before... [NPC] Marigold: However, a pesky Golden Goblin just came by and snatched a bunch of my Gold Essence! How can I operate safely with thieves like that running around? [NPC] Marigold: Speak to me again after killing a Golden Goblin and I'll show you my shop! I heard Golden Goblins have a small chance to spawn when mining ores or throwing Goblin Eggs in the Dwarven Mines or Crystal Hollows.|
|First Interaction; Already killed a Golden Goblin|[NPC] Marigold: Hey! I'm Marigold! I don't recall seeing you around these parts before... [NPC] Marigold: Now normally, I run a humble little shop that allows you to upgrade some useful perks in exchange for Gold Essence! [NPC] Marigold: However, a pesky Golden Goblin just came by and snatched a bunch of my Gold Essence! How can I operate safely with thieves like that running around? [NPC] Marigold: Speak to me again after killing a- [NPC] Marigold: wait... your Bestiary shows that you've already killed a Golden Goblin! [NPC] Marigold: I can't thank you enough! As a token of my gratitude, here's another 25 Gold Essence to get you started at my shop! It's not a lot, but a little goes a long way! [NPC] Marigold: Let me show you the Gold Essence Shop!|
|After killing a Golden Goblin|[NPC] Marigold: Amazing! Thanks for the help - those pesky Golden Goblins seem to appear all the time! [NPC] Marigold: Let me show you the Gold Essence Shop!|

|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving [[Midas Jewel]]|[NPC] Marigold: ✆ Wow, it's beautiful! [NPC] Marigold: ✆ I hope it wasn't too much of a hassle to get, I've heard stories of The Catacombs... [NPC] Marigold: ✆ Here's my contact, you can call me whenever you need to access the Gold Essence Shop. ✆ Marigold has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Marigold: ✆ Thanks again!|

Aliases: Fink

Master Tactician Fink is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Rift Dimension]]. They only appear in the [[Around Colosseum]] during [[Detective Amog]]'s quest, as well as in [[Taylor's]] during the second quest.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|During [[Detective Amog]]'s first reverse-quest|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Yes? Select an option: [We are investigating the future murder of Ramero.]|
|Selecting [We are investigating the future murder of Ramero.]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Really? Ramero is about to die? [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: What fantastic news! Select an option: [Are you the murderer?]|
|Selecting [Are you the murderer?]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: No, unfortunately I do not have it in me to do the deed myself. [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: I will gladly sell you the murder weapon however. Select an option: [That would be of great assistance...]|
|Selecting [That would be of great assistance...]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Help yourself.|
|Purchasing [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Nice doing business with you. I should talk to the detective now...|
|Already having purchased [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Good luck on solving the case. I should talk to the detective now...|
|During [[Detective Amog]]'s second reverse-quest|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Hello. [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Do you need anything else to solve your case? Select an option: [No, I already murdered Ramero.]|
|Selecting [No, I already murdered Ramero.]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Brilliant! [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: You did that so quickly! [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: I'm glad people like you exist! Select an option: [Have you stolen his body?]|
|Selecting [Have you stolen his body?]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Absolutely not. [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Why would I do that? Select an option: [I don't know.]|
|Selecting [I don't know.]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: So where is his body? [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Are you sure you killed him? Select an option: [Yes.]|
|Selecting [Yes.]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: That's very strange because I just saw you leave to the Blacksmith to find more clues. Select an option: [That's impossible, I went there a long time ago.]|
|Selecting [That's impossible, I went there a long time ago.]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: I swear. [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: You bought the Murder Weapon and left just a moment ago. [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Maybe you should go there and find out for yourself. [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: In any case, if Ramero is indeed dead, I have a place to be right now.|
|Before speaking to [[Detective Amog]] again|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: In any case, if Ramero is indeed dead, I have a place to be right now.|
|In [[Taylor's]]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: You lied to me! [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: I thought you murdered Ramero! Select an option: [I thought so too...]|
|Selected [I thought so too...]|[NPC] Master Tactician Fink: I look like a fool now. [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: I even sold you the murder weapon! [NPC] Master Tactician Fink: Really, you disappoint me.|

# Melancholic Viking
Aliases: Joyful Viking, Mournful Viking, Viking
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Melancholic Viking]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various items after completing a short quest to help them remember the sea.
Location: [[Viking Longhouse]]
Coordinates: -335.5 92.5 73.5
The Melancholic Viking is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Viking Longhouse]] by the [[Spruce Woods]]. He gives the player access to a special shop if they complete a short quest to remind him of the sea.
## Quest
These steps must be completed without leaving [[The Park]]. After two minutes, the quest will reset and the player will have to complete the quest again if they wish to access the shop again.
1. Use a [[Magical Water Bucket]] to extinguish the fire in the cabin. The water must be placed directly onto the fire.
2. Right-click either [[Furnace]] to the left of the Melancholic Viking with a [[Raw Cod]]. This will consume the fish, and make the furnace become active.
3. Use the [[Fish Hat]] Splash Ability. It must be used a total of 15 times, which costs 150 [[Mana]], for the Viking to finish reminiscing.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Melancholic Viking: I last saw the sea XX years, XX months ago. [NPC] Melancholic Viking: I wish I could remember what it felt like! [NPC] Melancholic Viking: Sadly, my memory is now my worst enemy. [NPC] Melancholic Viking: Please, help me remember the sea!|
|Putting out Fire with [[Magical Water Bucket]]|[NPC] Viking: YES! [NPC] Viking: Now that you poured an infinite amount of water, [NPC] Viking: I can remember the smell of salt water and the beauty of the waves. [NPC] Viking: If only I could recall the smell of a tasty supper with my old warband.|
|Putting out Fire with [[Water Bucket]]|You pour water from the bucket, but it's apparently never enough to extinguish the fireplace. [NPC] Viking: That's just... not enough water.|
|Holding [[Magical Water Bucket]]|[NPC] Viking: There's as much water here as an ocean. [NPC] Viking: If only you could pour it somewhere!|
|Before Cooking a [[Raw Cod]]|[NPC] Viking: I would like to smell a good feast!|
|Holding any [[Raw Cod]]|[NPC] Viking: I prefer when the fishes are lively and go splish-splash in the water!|
|Holding any Cooked Cod ([[Cooked Fish]])|[NPC] Viking: *takes a bite* [NPC] Viking: It definitely... *chomp* tastes good... [NPC] Viking: But the smell isn't quite right!|
|Cooking [[Raw Cod]]|[NPC] Viking: MMMMMMmmmMMMMH... [NPC] Viking: Smells so nice!|
|After cooking [[Raw Cod]]|[NPC] Viking: You know what I really remember from the sea? [NPC] Viking: Fishes splish-splashing around!|
|Starting to Splash ([[Fish Hat]])|[NPC] Viking: YES! This totally reminds me of the sea! [NPC] Viking: Although... there were more fishes back then.|
|Splashing ([[Fish Hat]]) more|[NPC] Viking: EXCELLENT! Just a few more fishes and splashes please!|
|Enough splashes ([[Fish Hat]])|[NPC] Viking: WOW! This feels just like on my Drakkar! [NPC] Viking: I suddenly feel so great! [NPC] Viking: Thanks for bringing joy to an old viking! [NPC] Viking: Take a look at my wares! (Interact with the NPC)|
|Splashing ([[Fish Hat]]) without cooking [[Raw Cod]] or having previously put out fire|[NPC] Viking: Wow! Nice move! There just isn't the ambiance to fully appreciate it.|
|Before putting the fire out|[NPC] Viking: If only there wasn't this infernal fire noise!|
|Cooking any [[Trophy Fish]]|[NPC] Viking: Ooh! That looks exotic! [NPC] Viking: It wasn't quite what I was looking for, but I'll take it thanks!|
|Holding [[Ice]] or [[Packed Ice]]|[NPC] Viking: Don't you have some liquid water?|
|Holding a [[Pufferfish Hat]]|[NPC] Viking: Ouch!|
|Holding a [[Fish Hat]]|[NPC] Viking: Awesome hat! Does it do anything special?|
|Holding a [[Viking's Tear]]|[NPC] Viking: Please don't tell my family that I cry sometimes!|
|Holding any Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]])|[NPC] Viking: It's a nice thought, but I don't feel like fishing right now.|
|Holding any Potion ([[Potions]])|[NPC] Viking: * sniffs * [NPC] Viking: Smells weird.|
|Holding any [[Boat]]|[NPC] Viking: Wow! [NPC] Viking: A boat! [NPC] Viking: Throw them at people you hate!|
## Trivia
- Interacting with the [[Furnace]] with a [[Trophy Fish]] for the first time grants the [[Achievements|"Meal fit for a King" Achievement]].
- The "Throw them at people you hate!" line when interacting with a boat is a reference to people used to be able to throw boats at others to fill their inventory to troll or sabotage them at the [[Dark Auction]].
- The Viking's "I last saw the sea # years, # months ago." line is based on the time since October 30th, 2009, or epoch time 1256960211193. This is 3,650 days before October 28th, 2019.
- Once all steps have been completed, lightning will rain down from the sky, and the Viking will become the Joyful Viking. The shop will be available for 2 minutes.

# Melody
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Quests, Quest giver
[Melody]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: The Rebuild ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to the [[Harp]].
Location: [[Melody's Plateau]]
Coordinates: -411.5 109 72.5
Melody is an [[NPC]] found in [[Melody's Plateau]] of the [[Savanna Woodland]]. She allows the player to play multiple songs on the [[Harp]] after repairing it with 512 (8 stacks) [[Acacia Log|Acacia Logs]]. Repairing it also rewards the player with [[Melody's Shoes]], 2,000 [[Coins]], +1,500 [[Foraging|Foraging XP]], and [[SkyBlock Levels|+5 SkyBlock XP]].
Upon completing a song, Melody can reward the player with [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], [[Intelligence]], and [[Melody's Hair]]. The player can also purchase a [[Personal Harp]] from her.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Melody ♫: Hello! ♪ Select an option: [Are you okay?]|
|After selecting [Are you okay?]|[NPC] Melody ♫: Yes, I'm fine, though my beloved Harp was broken to pieces by the storm. [NPC] Melody ♫: If you would be so willing, could you bring me the materials so that I may make another? [NPC] Melody ♫: My brother was on his way to help, but you got here first. [NPC] Melody ♫: If you'd be so kind as to bring me 512 Acacia Logs, I'll be able to do the rest.|
|Before bringing 512 [[Acacia Log]]|[NPC] Melody ♫: If you could bring me 512 Acacia Logs, I could craft another Harp!|
|After bringing 512 [[Acacia Log]]|[NPC] Melody ♫: Thank you so much! ❤ [NPC] Melody ♫: I already have some string, so I can just use this wood to fashion the base of the harp. [NPC] Melody ♫: Now, if I'm doing this right, this goes here, and... [NPC] Melody ♫: It worked! This harp looks and sounds even more beautiful than the last! ♪ [NPC] Melody ♫: Thank you for your help - please take this as a reward. [NPC] Melody ♫: Talk to me again if you ever want to give my Harp a try!|
|After Repairing the Harp|[NPC] Melody ♫: Hello! ♫ [NPC] Melody ♫: Want to learn the harp? [NPC] Melody ♫: I'll teach you! ❤ [NPC] Melody ♫: Pick a song to get started!|
|Completing all Songs|[NPC] Melody ♫: Wow! ❤ [NPC] Melody ♫: You perfected every Song! [NPC] Melody ♫: Maybe you should teach me now! [NPC] Melody ♫: Here's a gift so you always remember me! ♪|
|Getting 90% on all Songs|[NPC] Melody ♫: Wow! ❤ [NPC] Melody ♫: You mastered every song with over 90%! [NPC] Melody ♫: Maybe you should teach me now! [NPC] Melody ♫: Here's a gift so you always remember me! ♪|
## Tips
- Pressing drop item (Default keybind: q) instead of just clicking can help prevent high latency from causing rapid clicks to not be counted.
- By binding middle click to left or right mouse button (whichever you prefer) blocks will never be picked up when clicking, making missing clicks due to holding a block no longer a problem.
## Trivia
- Clicking when there is no note active will reduce your score even if you otherwise perfectly hit all notes.
- In total, the rewards are a permanent upgrade of +26 [[Intelligence]], [[SkyBlock Levels|+140 SkyBlock XP]], and the [[Melody's Hair]] accessory.

# Mining Merchant
Aliases: Mine Merchant
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Mining
[Mining Merchant]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells [[Mining]]-related items, including ores and [[Pickaxes]].
Location: [[Mining District]]
Coordinates: 14.5 63 -114
The Mining Merchant is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Mining District]] that sells [[Ores|Minerals]] and [[Pickaxes|Mining Tools]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Mining Merchant: My specialites are ores, stone, and mining equipment!|
## Trivia
- The [[Iron Forger]] and [[Gold Forger]] have separate stocks, meaning it is technically possible to buy up to 1,280 [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]] and [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]] per day.
- It is actually cheaper to buy [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]] from the [[Iron Forger]] and [[Gold Ingot|Gold Ingots]] from the [[Gold Forger]].

# Miria
Aliases: Mira, Miria's Milestone
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Miria]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to the island's Starlyn Contests ([[Starlyn Contest]]).
Location: [[Miria's Hut]]
Coordinates: -654.5 98 220.5
Miria is an [[NPC]] located in [[Miria's Hut]] within the [[Torrhus Canyon]] that hosts the island's [[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]].
## Contests
Miria hosts a contest that takes place every SkyBlock Day and lasts for 20 real-life minutes. During the contest, a player can gain points through various means.
## Shop
Miria's Shop has 5 milestones, which are progressed by collecting [[Miria's Coupon|Miria's Coupons]]. Each contest awards a certain amount of [[Miria's Coupon|Miria's Coupons]] based on rarity.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|After speaking to [[Helia]]|[NPC] Miria: Well nice to meet ya, <player>! [NPC] Miria: About time you made your way to Torrhus Canyon, Agatha has told me all about you! [NPC] Miria: So, now that you're here, are you ready to take on your next set of Starlyn Contests? Select an option: [Let's do it!] [Maybe later]|
|Selecting [Maybe later]|[NPC] Miria: Gonna have a look around the island first? No problem! Toodles!|
|Selecting [Let's do it!]|[NPC] Miria: Great! [NPC] Miria: Just so you know, my Starlyn Contests are partnered with the Critter Safari. [NPC] Miria: What that means is, whenever you reach the UNCOMMON bracket or higher in one of my contests, you'll get a free Safari Ticket alongside your usual rewards! [NPC] Miria: Show your ticket to the Safari Manager and he'll give you access to the Critter Safari for a limited time! [NPC] Miria: I go there quite often - it's a pretty cool place where you can hunt dozens of Critters to your heart’s content. [NPC] Miria: The better your ticket, the more free stuff you'll get, so aim for the stars! [NPC] Miria: #AD! [NPC] Miria: So, with all that in mind, are you ready to take on my Starlyn Contests? Select an option: [I sure am!] [I'll come back later]|
|Selecting [I'll come back later]|[NPC] Miria: See you around! Come back anytime!|
|Selecting [I sure am!]|[NPC] Miria: Come take a look at what my Starlyn Contests have to offer!|
|Reaching the MYTHIC bracket for the first time|[NPC] Miria: Huh? You reached my hidden MYTHIC bracket already? You're sure good at this Foraging business!|
|Reaching the DIVINE bracket for the first time|[NPC] Miria: Whaaat? You reached my hidden DIVINE bracket? Great job - keep it going!|
|Reaching the SPECIAL bracket for the first time|[NPC] Miria: You found my hidden SPECIAL bracket? Wow, my sister was right about you!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Holding an Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) and intereacting with Miria before reaching the LEGENDARY bracket in her contest at least once.|[NPC] Miria: ✆ Nice to see ya! [NPC] Miria: ✆ Hmm, you want my Abiphone Contact? [NPC] Miria: ✆ Sure, I guess I can do that... [NPC] Miria: ✆ But only if you reach the LEGENDARY bracket in my contest first!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Holding an Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]) and intereacting with Miria having already reached the LEGENDARY bracket in her contest at least once.|[NPC] Miria: ✆ Nice to see ya! [NPC] Miria: ✆ Hmm, you want my Abiphone Contact? [NPC] Miria: ✆ Sure, I guess I can do that... [NPC] Miria: ✆ But only if you reach the LEGENDARY bracket in my contest first! [NPC] Miria: ✆ ... [NPC] Miria: ✆ Wait... [NPC] Miria: ✆ You already reached it? [NPC] Miria: ✆ HOW? [NPC] Miria: ✆ Deal's a deal, I guess. [NPC] Miria: ✆ Here's my contact. [NPC] Miria: ✆ I'll give you a call whenever you finish a Miria's Contest so that you can claim your rewards. [NPC] Miria: ✆ ...Or you can call me anytime to view my Shop!|
## Notes
- Miria will only keep your rewards from previous contests for one Skyblock year.
## Trivia
- It requires 8,506 (132 stacks plus 58) [[Miria's Coupon]], 37 [[Miria's Prize]], 2,148 [[Enchanted Helix Log]] and 286 [[Helixis]] to max out the Miria's Shop. It includes +2 [[Foraging]] Level Cap, Miria's Power, Miria's Personal Best and [[Helix Log|Helix]] Fortune perks, 64 [[Woodpecker Shard]] and 48 [[Vulture Shard]], obtaining [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Sloth Pet]], [[Torrhus Artifact]], [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Petalfall|Petalfall V]]) and 2 [[Vial of Spring Water]].
  - This comes out to a total of if a player wishes to max out everything there.

# Moby
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Moby]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to the [[Chum Bucket]] and [[Chum]]. Also sells various [[Fishing]]-related items.
Location: [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]
Coordinates: 206 43 -500
Moby is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Glowing Mushroom Cave]]. They teach the player how to use a [[Chum Bucket|Empty Chum Bucket]] and have a shop for players to purchase fishing related items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First interaction|[NPC] Moby: So listen. [NPC] Moby: The other day I was chumming with my chums. [NPC] Moby: One of them yaps and I say, "ever tried my tonic"? [NPC] Moby: So yeah, I come back to my crib, right? [NPC] Moby: And listen, I've never seen anybody down here, but you seem like a nice fellow. [NPC] Moby: So just bring me 8 Glowing Mushrooms and I'll show you how to chum, alright buddy?|
|Bringing 8 Glowing Mushrooms ([[Glowing Mushroom]])|[NPC] Moby: Ok listen now, I practice a secret chumming technique. [NPC] Moby: It's reverse-chumming! [NPC] Moby: Instead of chumming, it's the sea creatures giving you Chums. [NPC] Moby: Listen, it's very easy. [NPC] Moby: First we trade a Chum Bucket, ok? [NPC] Moby: Just click me!|
|Before buying an Empty Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])|[NPC] Moby: Just click me!|
|After buying an Empty Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])|[NPC] Moby: First you place down the bucket near water, right? [NPC] Moby: Then use a fishing rod and throw a line in said water. [NPC] Moby: Ok and you wait now. That part is easier with YOUR chums. [NPC] Moby: Listen, time is shorter with friends. [NPC] Moby: After waiting you kill a sea creature and get a Chum. [NPC] Moby: You put it in the bucket and someday it's filled. [NPC] Moby: Then listen you bring me the filled bucket, ok?|
|Bringing a Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])|[NPC] Moby: Wow ok listen that's good. [NPC] Moby: You are the chumming expert ok? [NPC] Moby: Listen. I also have some good items for your chumming. [NPC] Moby: And I have this really good tonic. It's a really good tonic, ok? [NPC] Moby: So get mushrooms, fill chum buckets, trade tonics! Easy chums!|
|Not bringing the correct items|[NPC] Moby: Come back with the right amount of items!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Moby: Listen. Glowing Mushrooms don't seem to be too good for my health. About time I tried something else. You got anything, chum?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Moby: That's good.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Moby: You are no chum of mine.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Moby: ✆ Hey chum. Long time no see. [NPC] Moby: ✆ You want my Abiphone Contact? [NPC] Moby: ✆ Ok, listen. [NPC] Moby: ✆ I got all my chums in my phone, ok? [NPC] Moby: ✆ And we're chums too, you and I. [NPC] Moby: ✆ But I only got so many contact slots, you know? [NPC] Moby: ✆ And who am I to say which of my chums are the most important, you know? [NPC] Moby: ✆ What kind of chum would that make me? [NPC] Moby: ✆ So, if you want your digits in my phone, imma need you to get me something... Select an option: [What do you need?]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [What do you need?]|[NPC] Moby: ✆ What do you think it is? Select an option: [Is it chum?]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [Is it chum?]|[NPC] Moby: ✆ Of course it's chum, chum! [NPC] Moby: ✆ Bring me Full Chum Bucket x64 and I'll add you to my Abiphone! [NPC] Moby: ✆ Mates rates! [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without 64 (1 stack) Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])|[NPC] Moby: ✆ Hey chum, that's not it. [NPC] Moby: ✆ Ok, listen. [NPC] Moby: ✆ I asked for Full Chum Bucket x64. [NPC] Moby: ✆ Not whatever this is.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving 64 Full Chum Bucket ([[Chum Bucket]])|[NPC] Moby: ✆ Wow ok, that's good. [NPC] Moby: ✆ This is the stuff. The good stuff. [NPC] Moby: ✆ Here's my contact! ✆ Moby has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Moby: ✆ You ever need chumming supplies just give your chum Moby a call!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Moby: ✆ Hey chum!|
## Trivia
- Moby' name could be a reference to the sperm whale Moby Dick from Herman Melville's novel Moby-Dick.
  - Another item referencing this book is [[Moby-Duck]].
- Moby's skin could potentially be a reference to a similar skin made for the player GeorgeNotFound on the web series Dream SMP.

# Motes Grubber
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Motes Grubber]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Eats rift items in exchange for [[Motes]].
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -92.31 65 156.875
Description: Eats rift items in exchange for [[Motes]].
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -93 70.5 -73
Description: Eats rift items in exchange for [[Motes]].
Location: [[Stillgore Château]]
Coordinates: 164.5 77 125.5
Description: Eats rift items in exchange for [[Motes]].
Location: [[The Mountaintop]]
Coordinates: 1.37 114.781 21.375
Motes Grubber is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Wyld Woods]], [[West Village]], [[Stillgore Château]], and [[The Mountaintop]]. They eat rift items in exchange for [[Motes]]. Consuming [[McGrubber's Burger|McGrubber's Burgers]] increases the number of Motes offered by 5%, up to a maximum of 25%.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Motes Grubber: Me eat items, give Motes. [NPC] Motes Grubber: Before, me items, give Coins! [NPC] Motes Grubber: But now, me eat items, give Motes.|
## Tips
- It is not recommended to sell items to Motes Grubber, until the player has consumed all 5 [[McGrubber's Burger|McGrubber's Burgers]], as it increases the motes gain heavily.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:

|May 18, 2023|Added Motes Grubber to the [[Stillgore Château]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Motes Grubber.|

# Nemo
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Nemo]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells [[Snorkeling Armor]] and Respiration IV ([[Respiration (Enchantment)]]).
Location: [[Murkwater Shallows]]
Coordinates: -619.5 87 -6.5
Nemo is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Murkwater Shallows]] who sells [[Snorkeling Armor]] and [[Respiration (Enchantment)|Respiration IV]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Interaction|[NPC] Nemo: Have you explored... *inhales*... the depths of the lake yet? [NPC] Nemo: The deeper you venture... *exhales*... the greater the pressure becomes. [NPC] Nemo: It's essential... *inhales*... to equip yourself with ample... *exhales*... ⚶ Respiration and ❍ Pressure Resistance. [NPC] Nemo: Without these... *inhales*... accessing the lower chambers will be *exhales*... impossible.|
|Upon drowning in the [[Murkwater Depths]]|[NPC] Nemo: Ah, you're awake... *inhales*... I pulled you from the depths just in time. [NPC] Nemo: The pressure down there is... *exhales*... unforgiving. [NPC] Nemo: Visit my shop for ⚶ Respiration ...*inhales*... and ❍ Pressure Resistance gear. [NPC] Nemo: It’s essential for... *exhales*... surviving down there.|
|Upon drowning in the [[Murkwater Depths]]|[NPC] Nemo: And we meet again!... *inhales*... Seems the loch embrace is too strong? [NPC] Nemo: Perhaps lighten the load with some proper gear next time... *exhales*... just a thought.|
|Upon drowning in the [[Murkwater Depths]]|[NPC] Nemo: You're really testing the waters, aren't you... *inhales*... Literally. [NPC] Nemo: A bit more Respiration and... *exhales*... Pressure Resistance might stop the loch from swallowing you whole. [NPC] Nemo: Just... *inhales*... saying.|
|Upon drowning in the [[Murkwater Depths]]|[NPC] Nemo: You again... *inhales*... I’m no hero, but I might need a cape at this rate. [NPC] Nemo: Maybe equip yourself a bit better, and I can retire my rescue hat... *exhales*... just a suggestion.|
## Trivia
- Nemo wears a full set of [[Diver's Armor]] despite selling [[Snorkeling Armor]].
- Nemo is most likely named after the movie of the same name.

|Jun 5, 2025|Added Nemo.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Nuvian
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Crimson Isle
[Nuvian]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells [[Abiphones]] from [[Scarleton]].
Location: [[Dragontail]]
Coordinates: -693.5 102 -735.5
Nuvian is an [[NPC]] located in [[Dragontail]] that sells [[Abiphones]].
## Location
Nuvian is located within a room in the [[Dragontail]] brewery, on the [[Crimson Isle]]. They can only be accessed through a Keypad located at [[Coordinates|-678, 107, -736]], which requires a 4-digit code. The code can be obtained by overhearing [[Nall]] and [[Kutral]] talk through the window of the brewery, same building as the Keypad, at [[Coordinates|-655, 107, -741]]. Overhearing the conversation requires being beside the window, not directly in front of it, as well as standing still and crouching. The code changes every real life day.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Nuvian: Who are you and who gave you the code? [NPC] Nuvian: You are not supposed to be here. [NPC] Nuvian: To be fair, I'm not supposed to be here either. [NPC] Nuvian: If you are here for business then it makes no difference to me. [NPC] Nuvian: I sell Abiphones straight from the Mage faction.|
|During the Talk to 4 Villagers Quest ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 1/4)|[NPC] Nuvian: Are you a Wizard? [NPC] Nuvian: Well you don't look like one, and you don't smell like one either. [NPC] Nuvian: In that case, I have nothing against you. I wish you a sulphuric day sir.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 2/4)|[NPC] Nuvian: Hello stranger. Are you looking to join our faction? [NPC] Nuvian: What a fantastic choice! [NPC] Nuvian: We have a range of motives far deeper and more interesting than those terrible fire mages. [NPC] Nuvian: Joining our faction is quite hard but I'm sure an incredible individual like you will manage!|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 3/4)|[NPC] Nuvian: Sul-phur [NPC] Nuvian: Sulphur? [NPC] Nuvian: Me Sulphur.|
|(same as above) First Interaction (If they're Barbarian 4/4)|[NPC] Nuvian: Are you a Hero? [NPC] Nuvian: Did you come to free us from the everlasting threat of the wizards? [NPC] Nuvian: The mighty Volcano is protecting us, but for how long?|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Nuvian: You seem suspicious.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Nuvian: Sorry, I can't talk to you right now.|
|(same as above) After spoken to, or if 4/4 Barbarians are spoken to|[NPC] Nuvian: Please stop bothering me.|
## Trivia
- Nuvian's shop UI is titled Abiphone Shop, while most other vendors, including their [[Udel|Mage Faction counterpart]], have their own name.
  - Nuvian used to sell [[Abiphones]] for 1.5% more than their original prices offered by [[Udel]]; however, when [[Abiphones & Co.]] released, the prices were reduced to normal.
## History

|Aug 28, 2025|Removed 101.5% price multiplier from Nuvian.|
|Aug 28, 2025|Revamped shop.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 20, 2022|0.13|Added Nuvian.|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Removed 101.5% price multiplier from Nuvian.|
|Sep 2, 2025|0.23.4|Revamped shop.|

# Odawa
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Crystal Hollows
[Odawa]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various items in exchange for [[Sludge Juice]], including Jungle Keys ([[Jungle Key]]).
Location: [[Jungle]]
Odawa is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Jungle]] who lets the player buy various items in exchange for [[Sludge Juice]] and other assorted items.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Odawa: Welcome traveler to our village! [NPC] Odawa: We have much to offer and explore - but be careful. [NPC] Odawa: The Kalhuiki tribe are a friendly people, but protective. [NPC] Odawa: Do not attack our young, else we will put you down.|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Odawa: I'm supposed to collect some food for the village, the Kalhuiki people have been through a tough time recently.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Odawa: Perfect! I'll share the good news with my village!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Odawa: I guess I'll have to turn up empty-handed.|
## Trivia
- The name Odawa likely comes from the American indigenous people of the same name, roughly meaning "Trader".
- Using a [[Wishing Compass]] in the [[Jungle]] without a [[Jungle Key]] before obtaining a [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] may point to him.

# Odger
Aliases: Adept Trophy Fisher, Adept Trophy Hunter, Expert Trophy Fisher, Expert Trophy Hunter, Master Trophy Fisher, Master Trophy Hunter, Novice Trophy Fisher, Novice Trophy Hunter
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Odger]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]] Help Dean ([[Quests]]); Barbarian Training ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to [[Trophy Fish]].
Location: [[Odger's Hut]]
Coordinates: -373.5 207 -810.5
Odger is an [[NPC]] residing at [[Odger's Hut]] in the [[Crimson Isle]]. They provide various rewards for obtaining [[Trophy Fish]], in addition to being part of the [[Fishing]] quest for each [[Crimson Isle]] [[Factions|Faction]] questline.
## Mechanics
### Rewards
|Tier|Requirements|Rewards|
|-|-|-|
|Novice|Catch 15 Bronze tier or above [[Trophy Fish]].|[[Bronze Hunter Armor]] Odger's Bronze Tooth ([[Odger's Tooth]]) [[Bronze Trophy Fishing Sack]] 20 [[Magmafish]] [[Thunder]] Sea Creature|
|Adept|Catch Silver tier or above of all [[Trophy Fish]].|[[Silver Hunter Armor]] Upgrade Odger's Silver Tooth ([[Odger's Tooth]]) Upgrade [[Silver Trophy Fishing Sack]] 20 [[Silver Magmafish]] [[Lord Jawbus]] Sea Creature|
|Expert|Catch Gold tier or above of all [[Trophy Fish]].|[[Gold Hunter Armor]] Upgrade Odger's Gold Tooth ([[Odger's Tooth]]) Upgrade 20 [[Gold Magmafish]]|
|Master|Catch all tiers of all [[Trophy Fish]].|[[Diamond Hunter Armor]] Upgrade Odger's Diamond Tooth ([[Odger's Tooth]]) Upgrade [[Diamond the Fish]] 20 [[Diamond Magmafish]]|
### Filleting
[[Trophy Fish]] can be filleted by Odger in exchange for [[Magmafish]], the amount of which varies by type and quality.
Magmafish Rewarded per Filleted Fish:
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Quality BRONZE|Quality SILVER|Quality GOLD|Quality DIAMOND|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Blobfish|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|4|8|12|16|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Flyfish|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|32|48|64|96|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Golden Fish|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|400|700|1000|1300|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Gusher|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|32|48|64|96|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Karate Fish|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|40|60|80|120|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Lavahorse|RARE ([[Rarity]])|12|16|20|24|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Mana Ray|RARE ([[Rarity]])|40|60|80|120|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Moldfin|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|32|48|64|96|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Skeleton Fish|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|32|48|64|96|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Slugfish|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|40|60|80|120|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Soul Fish|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|32|48|64|96|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Steaming-Hot Flounder|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|20|28|40|60|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Sulphur Skitter|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|40|60|80|120|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Vanille|RARE ([[Rarity]])|80|120|160|240|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Volcanic Stonefish|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|20|28|40|60|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Obfuscated-1|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|16|24|32|48|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Obfuscated-2|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|40|60|80|120|
|[[Trophy Fish]]|Obfuscated-3|RARE ([[Rarity]])|400|700|1000|1300|
## Dialogue
Condition:
|First Interaction|[NPC] Odger: Crimson Isle fishing. Different. [NPC] Odger: Fish trophy like fish. But not same. [NPC] Odger: Unique way to fish. All trophy fish. [NPC] Odger: Bring trophy fish. To me. Reward. [NPC] Odger: Take this. Help you.|
|During [[Odexar]]'s quest|Select a topic: [Quest] [Trophy Fishing]|
|Selecting [Quest]; (With the Volcanic Stonefish ([[Trophy Fish]]) from [[Odexar]])|[NPC] Odger: This from Odexar? [NPC] Odger: Yep. Bad. [NPC] Odger: Small. Rare. Damaged. [NPC] Odger: Odexar no talent. No Style. [NPC] Odger: Find me better Trophy Fish. [NPC] Odger: Don't want to work on fish.|

|(same as above)|[NPC] Odger: Volcanic. Volcano.|

Old Shaman Nyko is an [[NPC]] found in the [[Howling Cave]]. They sell [[True Protection|True Protection I]] and [[True Essence]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Old Shaman Nyko: Where I live, vegetables aren't really an option. No sunlight, you see. Could you get me some?|

|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Old Shaman Nyko: Welp. Meat is good too, I suppose.|

# Ophelia
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dungeons
[Ophelia]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Sells items that can assist in the [[Catacombs]].
Location: [[Catacombs Entrance]]
Coordinates: -85.5 55 -139.5
Description: Sells items that can assist in the [[Catacombs]].
Location: [[Dungeon Hub]]
Coordinates: -49.5 119 -9.5
Ophelia is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Catacombs Entrance]] and the [[Dungeon Hub]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Ophelia: These Catacombs hold many riches, powerful items and great experience. [NPC] Ophelia: My trick? I make coins from the others' greed. [NPC] Ophelia: Are you interested in buying anything?|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Ophelia: ✆ Hmph. I don't know. [NPC] Ophelia: ✆ I'm really busy. [NPC] Ophelia: ✆ If I give you my contact it's strictly business, right? [NPC] Ophelia: ✆ I like collecting rings. There is one I've been wanting for a while now. [NPC] Ophelia: ✆ Have you heard of the Catacombs Expert Ring? [NPC] Ophelia: ✆ I really want it. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Ophelia: ✆ For real? [NPC] Ophelia: ✆ I haven't received a present like that...ever I think. [NPC] Ophelia: ✆ Here take my contact, and thank you!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Not Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Ophelia: ✆ You don't have what I need, nice try!|
|None; Before 0.7.11|[NPC] Ophelia: I'm currently setting up my shop, come back later!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Ophelia: Restocking, restocking!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Ophelia: Thanks! I'm headed back to my shop, hope to see you there soon!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Ophelia: I see. Hopefully next time I see you, you won't be needing anything that requires anything from me...|
## Trivia
- Ophelia sells [[Superboom TNT]] for 500 [[Coins]] more than [[Walter]].
- Whilst on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], Ophelia sold a range of [[Weapons]] and [[Armor]], as well as a [[Giant pile of Cash]], which sold to an [[NPC]] for 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]].
- Ophelia may be a reference to the character of the same name from William Shakespeare's play Hamlet.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Oringo Doesn't Leave After Event|Mmh... you shouldn't be able to click me right now!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Oringo: ✆ My contact?! [NPC] Oringo: ✆ What an unusual request! [NPC] Oringo: ✆ I travel a lot and I'm running quite low on Silent Pearl. [NPC] Oringo: ✆ If you bring me 64 of them, I'll give you my contact.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving 64 Silent Pearls ([[Silent Pearl]])|[NPC] Oringo: ✆ Great! Silent Pearls can be pretty useful sometimes.|

|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; [[Traveling Zoo]] Active|[NPC] Oringo: ✆ The Traveling Zoo has arrived! Here are my offerings this year: [NPC] Oringo: ✆ • <pet> Pet [NPC] Oringo: ✆ • <pet> Pet [NPC] Oringo: ✆ • <pet> Pet|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Oringo: Hey, you got some wheat for my pets by any chance? Gotta keep those little monsters fed, haha!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Oringo: You know how it is, they never stop eating haha, thank you very much!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Oringo: Oh, you're out of wheat? Alright, I'll come back later then.|
## Gallery
- Oringo's original skin
## Trivia
- The name "Oringo" is of African origin, and means “he who likes the hunt”.
- Oringo the Traveling Zookeeper used to have a different skin, but this was changed sometime after he was released.
- Calling Oringo on an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] will only list their current offerings. They will not sell other [[Pets]].

# Oruo The Almighty
Aliases: Oruo the Almighty, Sun Gecko Essence Shop
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters
[Oruo The Almighty]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to enter the [[Time Chamber]] to fight [[Sun Gecko]], or spend [[Sun Gecko Essence]] in the Sun Gecko Essence Shop ([[Oruo The Almighty]]).
Location: [[Continuum]]
Coordinates: 38 89 20
Oruo The Almighty is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Continuum]]. They allow the player to fight [[Sun Gecko]] and apply modifiers to the [[Time Chamber]]. They have an [[Essence Shops|Essence Shop]] where the player can purchase perks and items for [[Sun Gecko Essence]].
## Shop
[[Sun Gecko Essence]] can be used in the Sun Gecko Essence Shop by talking to Oruo The Almighty in the [[Continuum]].
|Perk / Item|Tier Ⅰ|Tier Ⅱ|Tier Ⅲ|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Combo Mania]]; Combos in the Time Chamber take +0.5 seconds to expire. Cost|50|||
|(same as above) Note|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|
|[[Extremely Real Shuriken]] (16); Can be thrown at Enemies to tag them. Grants +5% [[Magic Find]] when a tagged Enemy is killed. Cooldown: 1s Cost|32|||
|(same as above) Note|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|
|[[Grandma's Knitting Needle]]; Can be applied to Grandma Wolf Pets ([[Grandma Wolf Pet]]). Grants +5% [[Magic Find]] when getting a combo of 50 or above. Cost|50|||
|(same as above) Note|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|
|[[Super Leech Modifier]] (2); Can be combined with an [[Aspect of the Leech]] in a Crafting Table to increase its range from 4 blocks to 5 blocks and decrease its Mana Cost ([[Mana]]) from 60 to 50. Cost|25|||
|(same as above) Note|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|Can be purchased multiple times.|
|Blessing of Time||||

|Blessing of Time [[Mana Regen]]|+1 [[Mana Regen]]|+2 [[Mana Regen]]|+3 [[Mana Regen]]|
|Blessing of Time [[Strength]]|+2 [[Strength]]|+4 [[Strength]]|+6 [[Strength]]|

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Initial dialogue|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: I am Oruo The Almighty. I have lived many lives. I have ascended. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: I have control over the Blessing of Time. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: In this place where time has no meaning, I created a zone where time is everything. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: It is the only room in The Rift where your body will feel the effects of Time. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: In this Time Chamber, you will be able to train and get stronger. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Entry demands 5,000 Motes, a trivial sum for a taste of eternity. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Though beware, for your ф Rift Time shall deplete twice as fast. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: What say you? Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...]|
|Selecting [Maybe later...]|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: You only delay the inevitable.|
|After selecting Ponder with Oruo|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: A deity, you ponder? [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: I am but a seeker who stumbled upon the Blessing of Time hidden within the veils of this dimension.|
|After selecting Ponder with Oruo|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: My partner, Sun Gecko, and I discovered the Blessing of Time together. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: The fates decreed that only one could wield its power. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: It was I who was chosen, and thus I crafted this chamber so he could train against people such as yourself. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: He must become stronger, in preparation for a hardship that has yet to come.|
|After selecting Ponder with Oruo|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: The Precursors, you inquire? [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Through the Blessing of Time I have earned the knowledge and memories of the ancient God of Time. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: I have seen what no man has seen. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: The shadows of their return soon loom over us; we must stand prepared.|
|After selecting Ponder with Oruo|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Julian seeks me, yet he knows not the full scope of the web woven by Shen, his own master. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: If he finds his way through the labyrinth of time to my domain, it will be my duty to enlighten him. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: I will unveil the intricate plots of Shen and his organization. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: That knowledge, crucial and potent, may well tilt the scales of Destiny.|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] for the first time|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Magnificent. You have vanquished Sun Gecko, not a feat to be understated. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: While moments have passed for you, weeks have slipped by for Sun Gecko. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: He lies in rest, rejuvenated and eager for your return. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: The price for reentry remains at 5,000 Motes. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Dare you pay it? Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] for the second time|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Your strength prevails, but your journey has only just begun. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: You are now granted access to Time Chamber Modifiers and the Sun Gecko Essence Shop. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: The power of Sun Gecko is only limited by your own resolve. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Work the Modifiers of the Time Chamber, and your strength will know no bounds. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] later times|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Magnificent, you have vanquished Sun Gecko, not a feat to be understated. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Shall you challenge him again? Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] with 1 modifier active|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Once more you have triumphed over Sun Gecko. Such perseverance is rare, even in Tales of old. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] with 2 modifiers active|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: A third victory! You have surpassed even my expectations. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] with 3 modifiers active|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Though Sun Gecko's power grows more daunting, you have found a way through once again. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] with 4 modifiers active|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Sun Gecko's intensity thundered across the battlefield, yet your will stood firm, forging a triumph that whispers of greatness yet untold. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] with 5 modifiers active|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Sun Gecko pressed against the very limits of mortal endurance, yet today you sculpted a victory from raw chaos. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] with 6 modifiers active|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: As Sun Gecko unleashed deeper reserves of power, you carved a new mark of valor into the shifting sands of time. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After beating [[Sun Gecko]] with 7 modifiers active|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: At the apex of Sun Gecko's power, when all seemed unattainable, you forged a moment of legend that will echo through ages. Select an option: [Enter Time Chamber] [Maybe later...] [Ponder with Oruo]|
|After purchasing Blessing of Time I|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Your trials here have woven a portion of the Blessing of Time into the very fibers of your being. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Stronger you stand, bearing the vigor of both this realm and yours.|
|After purchasing Blessing of Time II|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Feel the surge of power coursing through your veins, a testament to your enduring spirit and the time spent within these walls. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: You have now absorbed a second measure of the Blessing of Time.|
|After purchasing Blessing of Time III|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: You have now received the final Blessing of Time. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Congratulations on mastering the trials of my Time Chamber. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: With the final Blessing, our contract is fulfilled, thrice granted, and thrice received. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Rest now, for you have earned it.|
|Not enough [[Rift Time]]|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: With less than 120ф Rift Time remaining, your risk outweighs the reward. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: I cannot permit you to enter. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Oruo's Time Chamber demands more than just fleeting moments.|
|Not enough Motes|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Your motes purse is not as strong as your will. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: I cannot permit you to enter. [NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Oruo's Time Chamber demands sacrifice.|
|Too far from the [[Continuum]]|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: In order to access the Time Chamber, you must be closer to its entrance. Please move closer to the Continuum.|
|[[Time Chamber]] disabled|[NPC] Oruo The Almighty: Interesting. The Time Chamber is not currently available.|
