# Pat
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Mining, Quest giver
[Pat]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: The Flint Bros ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells [[Gravel]] and [[Flint]].
Location: [[Graveyard]]
Coordinates: -87.5 73 -94.5
Pat is a [[Shops|Merchant]] [[NPC]] located in the [[Graveyard]]. They sell [[Gravel]] and [[Flint]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Pat: You like flint? I like flint! I sell flint! [NPC] Pat: My brother is mining the gravel from the Spider's Den. We are the Flint Bros!|
## Gallery
- Pat's house
- Pat's house prior to to 0.24.2
- The interior of Pat's house prior to 0.24.2
- Pat encased in purple glass
## Trivia
- His brother is [[Rick]], who is located at the [[Spider's Den]]. They are referred to as the "Flint Brothers" or "Flint Bros".
  - This is directed from the name of [[Quests|The Flint Bros]] quest from [[Rick]].
- Their looks and locations lead to believe that Rick and Pat are twin ghosts.
- Pat used to be incased in a Purple glass box. The reason for this is unknown.
- Pat can be found in the NPC menu of another Hypixel game, Housing, where Pat is called 'Ghostboy'.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Pat.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved Pat to a different location, due to redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Pearl Dealer
Aliases: End Shop, The End Shop

Type: [[NPC]]

Shop: ✔ Yes

The Pearl Dealer is an [[NPC]] located in [[The End]].
## The End Shop
Unlike other [[Shops|Merchants]], the player accesses their shop by clicking on displays next to the Pearl Dealer.
|Icon|Name|Cost|
|-|-|-|
|[[Gravity]]|[[Enchanted Book]] (Gravity VI ([[Gravity]]))|5,000,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Ender Slayer]]|[[Enchanted Book]] (Ender Slayer VI ([[Ender Slayer]]))|1,500,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[End Stone]]|[[End Stone]]|10 [[Coins]]|
|[[Obsidian]]|[[Obsidian]]|50 [[Coins]]|
|[[Remnant of the Eye]]|[[Remnant of the Eye]]|200,000 [[Coins]]|
|[[Stonk]]|[[Stonk]]|499,999 [[Coins]]|
|[[Silent Pearl]] x8|[[Silent Pearl]]|1,200 [[Coins]]|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: You have reached The End, though this is only the beginning. [NPC] Pearl Dealer: I am the Pearl Dealer, and you are on dangerous ground. [NPC] Pearl Dealer: Be careful when using Ender Pearls on this island, their energy attracts Endermen! [NPC] Pearl Dealer: The End also has many resources, including End Stone and Obsidian. [NPC] Pearl Dealer: The deeper you go, the stranger the things you'll find!|
|After Returning [[Dragon Shortbow]] to [[Lone Adventurer]]|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: You are [player] right? [NPC] Pearl Dealer: My friend told me about you! [NPC] Pearl Dealer: If you want a Dragon Shortbow I'll give you one for free! [NPC] Pearl Dealer: A good deed demands a proper reward!|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: Ender Pearls attract the attention of Endermen, but Silent Pearls don't! You can get them in my shop.|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: The End has endless End Stone and Obsidian. You may find a special type of these resources deep in the caves.|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: The items in my shop may be of help as you descend into the depths of The End.|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: Deep in these caves lays the nest of the Dragons, where you will find only peril. [NPC] Pearl Dealer: But you should try to find it anyway.|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: Talk to Guber up the staircase, he is a master of traversing this troubling terrain.|
|None; (Random)|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: Have you tried running The End Race yet? [NPC] Pearl Dealer: Talk to Guber to learn more. He is just up that staircase over there!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: My old buddy was a Wart Dealer...I miss him, and his Wart.|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: I'm glad that you're keeping him in your memories.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Pearl Dealer: His memory will soon be lost, like tears in rain.|

# Plumber Joe
Aliases: Joe, Plumber
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Plumber Joe]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the [[Plumber's Sponge]] and [[Super Scrubber]].
Location: [[Fishing Outpost]]
Coordinates: 123.5 74 -38.5
Plumber Joe is an [[NPC]] in the [[Village]]. He sells the [[Plumber's Sponge]] and [[Super Scrubber]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Plumber Joe: Water troubles? No problem!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Plumber Joe: I once dumped a magical water bucket into the toilet. It won't stop overflowing. Anyway, you got something for me?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Plumber Joe: Thanks. That should help me fix my water troubles.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Plumber Joe: I guess I'll just try to fix it myself.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Plumber Joe: ✆ My abiphone? You got it! [NPC] Plumber Joe: ✆ Call me during business hours and I'll help with your water troubles at a discount!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Daytime|[NPC] Plumber Joe: Water troubles? You know I'm on it!|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Night-time|✆ No answer.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling; Night-time|[NPC] Plumber Joe: ✆ Please only call me during business hours. [NPC] Plumber Joe: ✆ If there's really an emergency, come and see me!|
## Trivia
- Despite the mention of a discount, buying a [[Plumber's Sponge]] via the Abiphone Contact still costs the same as the usual price.
- Prior to the 0.19.9 update, business hours were based on the actual in-game time, and [[Day Saver]] and [[Night Saver]] had no effect on it. However, since the addition of the Island Time menu, setting your [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]] to day permanently allows you to call Plumber Joe anytime.

# Reed
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Reed]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the [[Rift Boat]].
Location: [[Lagoon Hut]]
Coordinates: -212 72 60.5
Reed is an [[NPC]] in the [[Lagoon Hut]] who sells the [[Rift Boat]].
## Quest
Reed asks for several items, which can be found in locations across the [[Rift Dimension]]. In return for bringing a group of items, Reed will reward the player with a permanent [[Rift Time]] boost.
|Items|Location|Reward|
|-|-|-|
|16x [[Larva Silk]]|[[Wyld Woods]]|20 [[Rift Time]]|
|64x [[Wilted Berberis]]|[[Dreadfarm]]|20 [[Rift Time]]|
|128x [[Half-Eaten Carrot]]|[[Village Plaza]]|30 [[Rift Time]]|
|1x [[Living Metal Anchor]]|[[Living Cave]]|30 [[Rift Time]]|
|8x [[Bacte Fragment]]|Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]])|30 [[Rift Time]]|
Upon completing all of Reed's tasks, the player will be able to purchase a [[Rift Boat]] for 25,000 [[Motes]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Reed: The crew and I have been stuck on this islet since we crossed the Rift. [NPC] Reed: We navigated through the infinite ocean to come here. [NPC] Reed: Alas, our boat broke into a million pieces when we were attacked by leeches. [NPC] Reed: Will you help me repair our boat?|
|Without Materials|[NPC] Reed: I'm still missing components to repair our boat. [NPC] Reed: Will you help me find them? [NPC] Reed: We need to get out of here!|
|Completing a Step|[NPC] Reed: Thanks, we're now one step closer to repairing the boat!|
|Completing all Steps|[NPC] Reed: Excellent! [NPC] Reed: Our boat is finally repaired! [NPC] Reed: Kay and I will soon escape the Rift. [NPC] Reed: Until then, I can make a boat for you using leftovers. [NPC] Reed: For a price of course... he he.|
|Completing all Steps; (Future Interactions)|[NPC] Reed: Thanks for delivering all the boat components. [NPC] Reed: Kay and I will soon escape the Rift. [NPC] Reed: In the meantime, I can make a boat for you using leftovers.|

# Researcher Ribery
Aliases: Adept Frog Collector, Expert Frog Collector, Master Frog Collector, Novice Frog Collector, Ribery
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Researcher Ribery]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to [[Trophy Frogs]].
Location: [[Lotus Atoll]]
Coordinates: -11.5 71 -6.5
Researcher Ribery is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Lotus Atoll]] that collects [[Trophy Frogs]].
## Mechanics
### Rewards
|Tier|Requirements|Rewards|
|-|-|-|
|Novice|Catch Bronze tier or above of all [[Trophy Frogs]].|20 [[Lotus]] [[Applicant's Statement]] [[Bronze Trophy Frog Sack]]|
|Adept|Catch Silver tier or above of all [[Trophy Frogs]].|10 [[Silver Lotus]] [[Student's Studies]] Upgrade [[Travel Scroll to the Lotus Atoll]] [[Silver Trophy Frog Sack]]|
|Expert|Catch Gold tier or above of all [[Trophy Frogs]].|5 [[Gold Lotus]] [[Master's Thesis]] Upgrade|
|Master|Catch all tiers of all Trophy Frog.|[[Diamond Lotus]] [[PhD's Grimoire]] Upgrade [[Ribbit the Fish]]|
### Donating
[[Trophy Frogs]] can be donated to Researcher Ribery in exchange for [[Lotus]], the amount of which varies by type and quality.
Lotus Rewarded per Donated Frog:
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Quality BRONZE|Quality SILVER|Quality GOLD|Quality DIAMOND|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Common Frog|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|8|16|32|64|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Exploding Frog|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|12|24|48|96|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Leap Frog|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|24|48|96|192|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Wetlands Frog|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20|40|80|160|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Reality Hopper|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20|40|80|160|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Blessed Frog|RARE ([[Rarity]])|32|64|128|256|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Bullfrog|RARE ([[Rarity]])|40|80|160|320|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Sea Frog|RARE ([[Rarity]])|40|80|160|320|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Cave Frog|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|80|160|320|640|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Highlands Frog|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|80|160|320|640|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Tree Frog|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|80|160|320|640|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Puddle Jumper|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|128|192|256|512|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Researcher Ribery: Welcome to the Lotus Atoll! [NPC] Researcher Ribery: Us Researchers are currently hard at work documenting the various species of Frog found here. [NPC] Researcher Ribery: Want to give us a hand? Select an option: [I suppose I don't have a choice in the matter.]|
|Selecting [I suppose I don't have a choice in the matter.]|[NPC] Researcher Ribery: Awesome attitude! [NPC] Researcher Ribery: There are 12 species of Trophy Frogs that can be fished around the Lotus Atoll. [NPC] Researcher Ribery: Each species comes in four rarities - BRONZE, SILVER, GOLD, and DIAMOND. [NPC] Researcher Ribery: That means there are 48 unique Trophy Frogs to find! |Selecting [What's in it for me?]|[NPC] Researcher Ribery: If you bring any Frogs you catch to me, I'll reward you with some Lotus! [NPC] Researcher Ribery: Progress through your Lotus Collection to unlock new useful items! Select an option: [Okay, I'm listening...]|

# Richard
Aliases: Stock Exchange, Stonks Auction
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Richard]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Hosts auctions for [[Stock of Stonks]].
Location: [[Trade Center]]
Coordinates: -32.5 70 -80.5
Richard is an [[NPC]] in the [[Trade Center]] that hosts auctions for [[Stock of Stonks]]. Auctions are held every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Month]], provided that [[Diaz]] is elected with her Stock Exchange perk.
## Stonks Auctions
1 → 25 [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonks]] can be purchased from Richard's [[Richard|Stonks Auction]], depending on the amount of [[Coins]] bid and placement relative to other players, with a minimum of 2,000,000 [[Coins]].
|Placement|Amount of Stocks of Stonks ([[Stock of Stonks]])|
|-|-|
|Top 100|25|
|Top 500|15|
|Top 1,000|10|
|Top 2,500|5|
|Top 5,000|2|
|Everyone else|1|
### Timing
Since [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] end on 27th of Late Spring of every SkyBlock year:
- 13 Stonks Auctions take place, the majority lasting 10 hours and 20 minutes.
- the 1st one ends a mere 80 minutes after [[Diaz]] is elected
- the 13th and last one ends on 27th of Late Spring of the following year, making it 540 minutes, or 80 minutes shorter than the majority of the auctions.
Stonks Auctions Schedules (assuming perk is active):
|In-Game Date|Starting Time of Auctions|
|-|-|
|27th of Late SpringLasts 80 minutes|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Summer|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Autumn|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late Winter|: Countdown:|
|1st of Early Spring|: Countdown:|
|1st of Spring|: Countdown:|
|1st of Late SpringLasts 9 hours|: Countdown:|
|End of [[Diaz]]'s term and last Auction|: Countdown:|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|When Diaz is not in office|[NPC] Richard: ... [NPC] Richard: Can't you see I'm busy? [NPC] Richard: Oh, you can't? [NPC] Richard: Whatever. Go talk to Liz, she'll tell you what's up.|
|First Interaction when Diaz is in office|[NPC] Richard: Hey hey heyyy! [NPC] Richard: I'm Richard, the Stonks Auction Master. [NPC] Richard: As long as Diaz is Mayor, I'll let you bid on Stock of Stonks to use in Liz's shop! [NPC] Richard: It's kind of a big deal.|
## Trivia
- Excluding [[Golden Bounty]], utilizing all of [[Liz]]'s products to max potential requires 338 (5 stacks plus 18) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]] from Richard.
  - Total of 116 (1 stack plus 52) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]] are required to get [[Freshly-Minted Coins]] for both [[Relic of Coins]] and [[Scavenger Artifact]].
  - Additional 72 (1 stack plus 8) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]] are required to upgrade all items using [[Golden Fragment|Golden Fragments]].
  - And 150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Stock of Stonks|Stocks of Stonk]] are required for both [[Crown of Avarice]] and [[Emerald Artifact]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 3, 2024|Added Richard.|

# Sanger
Aliases: Elusive Shop, Elusive shop
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Sanger]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Sells Elusive Shards ([[Attributes]]).
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -632.5 94 160.5
Description: Sells Elusive Shards ([[Attributes]]).
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -746.5 93 261.5
Description: Sells Elusive Shards ([[Attributes]]).
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -526.5 97 218.5
Sanger is an [[NPC]] located on the [[Torrhus Canyon]] at a different point every [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]]. They sell [[Elusive]] [[Attributes|Shards]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Sanger: Hello there, my dear fellow! [NPC] Sanger: It looks like you've found me! [NPC] Sanger: I'm Sanger - an elusive man who sells Shards gathered from Elusive mobs... Select an option: [What are Elusive Shards?]|
|Selecting [What are Elusive Shards?]|[NPC] Sanger: Good question. [NPC] Sanger: To put it simply, if you find a mob with the Elusive type... [NPC] Sanger: And you go ahead and kill that mob... [NPC] Sanger: It will ALWAYS drop its Shard! [NPC] Sanger: No ifs, no buts! No getting unlucky - a Shard is GUARANTEED! [NPC] Sanger: Simple as that! Do you understand, my dear fellow? Select an option: [Yep!] [Can you give me an example?]|
|Selecting [Can you give me an example?]|[NPC] Sanger: Most certainly! [NPC] Sanger: Special Zealots have the Elusive type... [NPC] Sanger: That means, whenever you kill a Special Zealot, it will always drop a Special Zealot Shard. [NPC] Sanger: Understood? Select an option: [Yep!] [Can you give me another example]|
|Selecting [Can you give me another example]|[NPC] Sanger: Of course! [NPC] Sanger: The Titanoboa has the Elusive type... [NPC] Sanger: That means, whenever you kill a Titanoboa, it will always drop a Titanoboa Shard. [NPC] Sanger: Understood? Select an option: [Yep!] [Can you give me one last example?]|
|Selecting [Can you give me one last example?]|[NPC] Sanger: I suppose I can... [NPC] Sanger: The Field Mouse has the Elusive type... [NPC] Sanger: That means, whenever you kill a Field Mouse, it will always drop a Field Mouse Shard. [NPC] Sanger: I hope you're beginning to understand now... Select an option: [Yep!]|
|Selecting [Yep!]|[NPC] Sanger: Even the best Hunters around struggle to find Elusive mobs... [NPC] Sanger: However, thankfully for you, I am a particularly celebrated Hunter who has traveled the land far and wide. [NPC] Sanger: I have collected many Shards in my time, gathered from the Elusive mobs that frequented my homeland. [NPC] Sanger: Why don't you peruse my collection - perhaps we can strike a deal?|
|Closing shop interface|[NPC] Sanger: Come back anytime! [NPC] Sanger: Though, you might have to find me again.|
|After finding them again|[NPC] Sanger: Oh, how brilliant! You appear to have found me once again! [NPC] Sanger: There truly is no tricking you, is there?|
|In shop upon a new day|Sanger seemed to vanish before your very eyes!|
## Trivia
- It costs 104,000,000 [[Coins]] and 7, 7, 8, 8 and 11 days to upgrade each [[Attributes|Attribute]] sold to level X.
  - This can be reduced up to 100,880,000 [[Coins]] with [[Seal of the Family]] accessory line.

# Shaggy
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters
[Shaggy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Shaggy's Quest ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Introduces the player to [[Arachne]].
Location: [[Grandma's House]]
Coordinates: -278.5 121 -182.5
Shaggy is an [[NPC]] located on the top floor of [[Grandma's House]] in the [[Spider's Den]].
## Quest
Shaggy first tasks the player with defeating [[Arachne]]. After that, he allows the player to purchase the [[Arachne Crystal]] for 2 [[Arachne Fragment|Arachne Fragments]], 16 [[Enchanted Spider Eye|Enchanted Spider Eyes]], and 16 [[Enchanted String]].
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Shaggy: I used to think this world was peaceful, but there are some beings that are stronger than I could ever hope to handle... [NPC] Shaggy: This spider, Arachne - it's way out of my league... [NPC] Shaggy: I can barely defeat her Keepers...|
|Before Getting Any [[Arachne's Calling]]|[NPC] Shaggy: With enough Arachne's Calling, you can summon her at the Altar.|
|Before Getting Any [[Arachne's Calling]]|[NPC] Shaggy: You can find Arachne's Keepers in Arachne's Sanctuary.|
|Having defeating [[Arachne]]|[NPC] Shaggy: Wow, you actually challenged that old witch Arachne! You have some potential kid... [NPC] Shaggy: Unfortunately, she has fled to bide her time and grow in power. [NPC] Shaggy: In order to prevent her from growing too strong, we must keep pulling her into this world and weakening her. [NPC] Shaggy: If you're up for the challenge, I can trade you some Arachne Crystals. [NPC] Shaggy: When you place one of these crystals at the Altar you'll pull a stronger version of Arachne into this world. [NPC] Shaggy: While she may drop some rarer items upon defeat, she is a lot stronger. [NPC] Shaggy: Here, take a look.|
|Bringing Required Materials|[NPC] Shaggy: Great, this is everything I need! With this I can make the crystal. [NPC] Shaggy: * Hammer and drilling sounds * [NPC] Shaggy: * Glass breaking * [NPC] Shaggy: Here ya go, if you put this on the alter, it will drag that retched creature to this world. [NPC] Shaggy: But be warned, she is strong, and I have it set up to drag her back if she is not defeated in 10 minutes, for the safety of this whole world. [NPC] Shaggy: I wish you good luck!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Nice to see you again. [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Hm, you want my Abiphone Contact? [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ That's fair, I suppose. [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Before I give you it, I ask but one thing of you... Select an option: [What is it?]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [What is it?] Before Killing [[Arachne]]|[NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Defeat Arachne. [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ She lives deep underground, in Arachne's Burrow. [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Help take her down and save this island from a horrible fate, and you can have my contact.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Selecting [What is it?] After Killing [[Arachne]]|[NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Kill Arachne. [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ .. [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Wait.. [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ You've... already killed her? [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Amazing! Great job! [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ The island is safe! For now, at least... [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Here, take my contact! ✆ Shaggy has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Shaggy: ✆ Call me anytime you need to get your hands on some Arachne Crystals!|

The Spider Tamer is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Arachne's Sanctuary]] of the [[Spider's Den]] who has a questline and offers the [[Essence Shops|Spider Essence Shop]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Spider Tamer: Oh...Hi. [NPC] Spider Tamer: I like staying here with my spiders, but Arachne started capturing some of them. [NPC] Spider Tamer: Can you help?|
|Killed [[Arachne]]|[NPC] Spider Tamer: Thank you so much... [NPC] Spider Tamer: If you want, I can help you gain the strength of spiders!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Hey dude. [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ I'm feeling kind of down at the minute. [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ All of my pet spiders left me to go and worship Arachne. [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ I don't blame them, it looks pretty exciting down there, but it's got kinda lonely up here as a result, you know? [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Hey, do you think you could give me a [Lvl 1] Tarantula? [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ I'm sure one of those little guys would keep me company, at least for a while. [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ If they wanna go worship Arachne when they're all grown up, that's cool with me but, until then, we could be great friends. [GIVE PET]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Without EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Tarantula Pet]]|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Uh, dude, you don't have a [Lvl 1] Tarantula. [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ I'd know, I'm quite the expert on our eight-legged friends - they don't call me the Spider Tamer for nothing!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Giving EPIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Tarantula Pet]]|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Thanks dude! [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ This might just be the cutest Tarantula I've ever seen! [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Look! She's looking at me with each of her eight eyes, how cute! [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ I think I'll name her Aragog. [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Anyways, here, take my contact. ✆ Spider Tamer has been added to your Abiphone's contacts! [NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ If you ever need to use the Spider Essence Shop or just want to hear some spider fun facts, hit me up!|
|Secondary Addition|Random Dialogue|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Did you know? Tarantulas can get so big that they can prey on some birds, and even lizards!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Did you know? Spiders are arachnids, not insects!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Did you know? Most spiders have blue blood.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Did you know? Spiders don't have an internal skeleton.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Did you know? Spiders sense their surroundings through vibrations and their sensitive leg hairs.|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Calling|[NPC] Spider Tamer: ✆ Did you know? Most spiders liquify their food! That's how they can eat stuff that's bigger than them!|

# Station Master
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Dwarven Mines
[Station Master]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to travel to various parts of the [[Dwarven Mines]] in exchange for [[Coins]].
Location: [[Dwarven Village]]
Coordinates: 38.5 201 -85.5
The Station Master is an [[NPC]] in the [[Dwarven Village]] that allows players to easily travel around the [[Dwarven Mines]] at a cost. The cost of the minecart ride is calculated by multiplying the distance to the destination by 2x.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Station Master: Hop in and we'll be off!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Station Master: See that empty, weak metal box? Good! That's your cart, hop in!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Station Master: Hold tight, it's going to be a bump ride!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Station Master: Thanks for choosing Dwarven Mine Co. - we operate 24/7 across the whole mine! [NPC] Station Master: Now, please climb aboard before your cart leaves without you. [NPC] Station Master: Again.|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Station Master: Let's a go!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Station Master: Ever been in one of these before? No? [NPC] Station Master: Well... [NPC] Station Master: I'm sure you'll be fine!|
|Purchasing a Ride|[NPC] Station Master: Last guy who was in this cart didn't feel too well so watch out he might have left a surprise in there.|
|Missed the [[Minecart]]|[NPC] Station Master: Quickly now the Minecart is leaving! Oh no! It looks like you missed the Minecart!|
|Arriving in [[Dwarven Village]]|[NPC] Station Master: Welcome to the Dwarven Village!|
|Traveling to [[Hanging Court]]|[NPC] Station Master: Off to the court? I would tell you to talk to the King but I don't know who he is anymore...|
|Traveling to [[Hanging Court]]|[NPC] Station Master: Did you know the Court is almost as old as the throne room, it's got some lovely flower beds too!|
|Traveling to [[Gates to the Mines]]|[NPC] Station Master: See those statues? They are the former Kings, Bunumir Ironbeard and Skordous Pebblebeard, guarding the entrance to the mines.|
|Traveling to [[Aristocrat Passage]]|[NPC] Station Master: Aristocrat passage ey? Best of luck to you.|
|Traveling to [[Forge Basin]]|[NPC] Station Master: Did you know that the Forge is so big it has to have multiple forging stations.|
|Traveling to [[Goblin Burrows]]|[NPC] Station Master: You ever seen those Goblins? Some say they lay eggs. How weird.|
|Traveling to [[Goblin Burrows]]|[NPC] Station Master: Goblin's are not the only creatures that dwell here. Rumor has it dirt monsters linger in these parts too...|
|Traveling to [[Abandoned Quarry]]|[NPC] Station Master: Fell into a state of disrepair following an accident.|
|None|[NPC] Station Master: I once stumbled upon a hidden lake in these parts, Some unsavory characters living there robbed me of all my gold!|
|None|[NPC] Station Master: You came down the lift? I never thought you could get this far down! Someone should have called this place the Deeper Caverns!|
|None|[NPC] Station Master: Hey, if you find any Titanium down here let me know! I wanted to buy some of that stuff for a new pickaxe.|
## Trivia
- It is impossible to leave the Minecart until having reached the destination, even by pressing ⇧ Shift.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Station Master.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Station Master.|

# Teddy
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters
[Teddy]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Teaches the player how to Hunt ([[Hunting]]) certain creatures.
Location: [[North Reaches]]

Teddy is an [[NPC]] found in the [[North Reaches]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]] that teaches the player how to [[Hunting|Hunt]] several different creatures.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Teddy: I am Teddy, the legendary hunter. Yes, yes, I know what you're thinking... no need to bow. [NPC] Teddy: I've walked this earth for 152 years, seen every creature, caught every beast... except... anyways. [NPC] Teddy: I can teach you how to catch anything that walks, swims, or slithers on this island... Select an option: [Wow, thanks!]|
|After selecting [Wow, thanks!]|[NPC] Teddy: ...For a price, of course. Nothing in life is free, kiddo.|
|Catching Mossybits|[NPC] Teddy: Struggling with Frogs? Really? [NPC] Teddy: Back in my day, hunters would catch 'em blindfolded. [NPC] Teddy: Hunters these days... no patience. Go to the lake and look for Mossybits. [NPC] Teddy: Just poke at 'em, and they'll jump like they've seen a ghost. Eventually, they'll tire out. [NPC] Teddy: Easy peasy.|
|Catching Mudworms|[NPC] Teddy: Mudworms? You've gotta watch the ground closely... and I mean closely. [NPC] Teddy: Ever seen a faint glow in the dirt? Could be treasure... or a Mudworm. [NPC] Teddy: Either way, you're digging!|
|Catching Coralots|[NPC] Teddy: Ah, the Axolotl, tricky little devils. But here's a secret... [NPC] Teddy: Their eyesight is rubbish, they only detect movements. So if you stand still long enough near them, you're golden.|
|Catching Hideonleafs|[NPC] Teddy: Shulkers are sneaky. They're like shadows that play tricks on you. [NPC] Teddy: There are Hideonleafs on the island. [NPC] Teddy: Step on them by mistake, and, well, they can get quite angry. [NPC] Teddy: When they attack, smack their projectiles right back at 'em. That'll teach 'em.|
|Catching Lumisquids|[NPC] Teddy: Lumisquids hide in awkward places… but that's half the fun, right? [NPC] Teddy: Use your net on them until they shrink down. [NPC] Teddy: They'll poof away like magic, but they never go too far.|
|Catching Joydives|[NPC] Teddy: Dolphins... smart and slippery. They'll flee to their dolphin holes if you're not quick enough. [NPC] Teddy: Get that net ready, and don't stop until you catch up to them. But hurry, they won't wait around for a chat.|
|Catching Cod|[NPC] Teddy: Cod? Oh, please. The most boring of fish, hands down. [NPC] Teddy: Spin 'em with a fishing net; they'll twirl like a clumsy dancer. [NPC] Teddy: Spin 'em too much, though, and... well, you'll see.|
|Catching Bambuleafs and Mochibears|[NPC] Teddy: Pandas? Oh, now we're talking real game! [NPC] Teddy: They're tough to find, but this old hunter knows a trick or two. [NPC] Teddy: Feed 'em bamboo, and they might just love you for a second. [NPC] Teddy: But finding them? [NPC] Teddy: That's a secret only a 165-year-old like me can tell you. I won't though.|
|Catching Salmon|[NPC] Teddy: Salmon? Fast as lightning, those slippery devils. [NPC] Teddy: Hit 'em with the net to slow them down. Just don't blink, or you'll be left with water in your boots.|
|Catching Invisibugs|[NPC] Teddy: Ah, the Invisibug, nearly impossible to see... but not to me, of course. [NPC] Teddy: Look for the glow, throw a lasso, and hope your aim's as sharp as mine.|
|Catching Shellwises|[NPC] Teddy: Shellwise, the ancient ones. [NPC] Teddy: Well, ancient compared to you, not me, I'm only 178, after all. [NPC] Teddy: No real tricks here. Just chuck your net until they give up. [NPC] Teddy: It's not pretty, but it works.|
|Catching Spikes|[NPC] Teddy: Pufferfish... Have you tried looking in the Kelpwoven Tunnels? [NPC] Teddy: I've seen a few, Spikes they are called. [NPC] Teddy: You'll need a special Fishing Net with a long range and stand at a distance from them.|
## Interface
Teddy/UI
## History

|Jun 5, 2025|Added Teddy.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Teddy.|

Terry is an [[NPC]] located in [[Terry's Shack]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]]. They sell fishing-related [[Reforge Stones]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Terry: Ahoy, <player>! Welcome to the Jerry Pond! [NPC] Terry: You may have noticed that this pond is full of unique creatures and treasures! [NPC] Terry: I'm looking for Ice Hunks and Blue Ice Hunks! [NPC] Terry: You can find these by ice fishing in the pond - break the ice and cast your rod! [NPC] Terry: Bring enough hunks back to me and I will reforge your fishing rod with modifiers exclusive to me - Terry!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Terry: Ahoy, <player>! People always tell me they like ice cream, but ice never really tastes like anything for me!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Terry: You are saying I need to combine these items with ice to have a taste? I'll believe it when I see it!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Terry: I'll keep crunching on those ice cubes, maybe I am missing something.|

[NPC] Terry: ✆ Ice Hunks and Blue Ice Hunks won't cut it anymore, unfortunately. [NPC] Terry: ✆ If you want me in your phone, bring me a Winter Water Orb! [GIVE ITEM]|

# Tomioka
Aliases: Tomika, Tomoika
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Tomioka]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells higher-tier Enchanted Books ([[Enchanted Book]]).
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Tomioka is an [[NPC]] that has a chance to spawn in any floor of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]. They sell higher-tier [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Tomioka: I ordered a snack down to my room in the Catacombs. Took them years to find me, and even then they forgot the toppings!|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Tomioka: Sometimes you just have to take matters into your own hands, you know?|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Tomioka: I didn't expect you of all people to let me down...|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Tomioka: ✆ If I have an Abiphone? [NPC] Tomioka: ✆ Very funny. [NPC] Tomioka: ✆ My great-great grandfather invented Abiphones. [NPC] Tomioka: ✆ I could really use two, no just one Titanic Experience Bottle. [NPC] Tomioka: ✆ Sorry for the odd request, but those are very hard to get around here. [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Tomioka: ✆ Ah, just what I needed!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Not Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Tomioka: ✆ You don't have what I need! Nice try!|
## Trivia
- Upon buying an item from Tomioka, players will receive the [[Achievements|"Rough Deal" Achievement]].
- There is a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] located under the stairs that lead up to Tomioka.
- Tomioka requesting 2 [[Titanic Experience Bottle|Titanic Experience Bottles]] could reference that that is approximately what would be needed to apply all of their enchantments to items.
- Tomioka's dialogue could suggest a link between the [[Crimson Isle]] and the [[Catacombs]] as both [[Maddox the Slayer]] (who is implied to have invented over-the-air contact) and [[Udel]] (Abiphone salesman) are from [[Scarleton]].

# Tony
Aliases: Terry's Shop, Tony's Shop
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Farming
[Tony]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells Tier XII [[Farming]] [[Minions]].
Location: [[Trapper's Den]]
Coordinates: 278.5 104 -544.5
Tony is an [[NPC]] in the [[Trapper's Den]]. They sell Tier XII [[Farming]] [[Minions]] in exchange for the same minion at Tier XI, 75 [[Pelts]] and a number of the associated materials.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]])|[NPC] Tony: ✆ Yo bro, how's it going? [NPC] Tony: ✆ Such lovely weather in this little ol' cave today, isn't it? [NPC] Tony: ✆ I guess you want my Abiphone Contact, huh? Select an option: [Yes]|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Tony: ✆ Well, I'll give you it if you play a game with me. [NPC] Tony: ✆ Not much else to do around here, really. Select an option: [What kind of game?]|
|Selecting [What kind of game?]|[NPC] Tony: ✆ How about you guess my favorite animal? [NPC] Tony: ✆ I'll let you know if you get it right.|
|Sending "Horse" in chat|[NPC] Tony: ✆ Haha yeah, my favorite animal is a horse. [NPC] Tony: ✆ Nice one bro. [NPC] Tony: ✆ Well, I'm a man of my word. Here's my contact. ✆ Tony has been added to your Abiphone's contacts!|
## Trivia
- It would take 1,125 [[Pelts]] to purchase every minion from Tony. If the player constantly gets a new animal every minute, it would take anywhere between 18 hours and 1 hour, depending on the player's luck.
- Tony's Shop was originally called Terry's Shop, but was later changed to avoid confusion with [[Terry]], an [[NPC]] on the [[Jerry's Workshop|Winter Island]].

# Treasure Hunter
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Quest giver
[Treasure Hunter]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells hints to treasure chests throughout the [[Mushroom Desert]].
Location: [[Archaeological Site]]
Coordinates: 200.5 92 -437
The Treasure Hunter is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Archaeological Site]] that offers the player quests to find treasure chests located throughout the [[Mushroom Desert]]. The player must pay a certain amount of [[Coins]] to start a quest, with the treasure rewards increasing in value based on the amount of [[Coins]] paid.
## Treasure
After paying a certain amount of [[Coins]], the player will receive a hint as to the location of the treasure. Finding the treasure will reward the player with an item.

|1,000 [[Coins]]|5 [[Enchanted Brown Mushroom]]|7,000 [[Coins]]|

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Treasure Hunter: Hello adventurer! [NPC] Treasure Hunter: I'm the Treasure Hunter, I've been searching for treasure all over this island. [NPC] Treasure Hunter: Rumor has it that there are rare stones and other valuable bounty hidden in the ground all over the island. [NPC] Treasure Hunter: Tell you what, for a small price I'll give you the info I know about. Pay # coins for the info? Click an option: [YES] - [NO]|
|Selecting [NO]|[NPC] Treasure Hunter: If you change your mind, you know where to find me!|
|Selecting [YES]|[NPC] Treasure Hunter: [hint]|
|Selecting [YES] Without Enough [[Coins]]|[NPC] Treasure Hunter: Are you trying to scam me? [NPC] Treasure Hunter: You don't have enough money. [NPC] Treasure Hunter: Come back when you have enough for the tip.|
|After Receiving Tip|[NPC] Treasure Hunter: Did you forget the tip? [NPC] Treasure Hunter: Just for you I'll tell you it again at no charge! [NPC] Treasure Hunter: [previous hint] [NPC] Treasure Hunter: Remember, you need to DIG to find the treasure!|
|On Cooldown|[NPC] Treasure Hunter: Sorry I dont have any more info. [NPC] Treasure Hunter: Come back in 3 SkyBlock days and I might know something new.|
|After Cooldown|[NPC] Treasure Hunter: Glad to see you again adventurer! [NPC] Treasure Hunter: You know the drill, for a small fee I will give you some info on the treasure. Pay # coins for the info? Click an option: [YES] - [NO]|
## Trivia
- The cooldown lasts 3 SkyBlock days, which is 60 real-life minutes.
- The Treasure Hunter is mentioned in [[Mason]]'s dialogue.
- The treasure will be moved into the player's inventory automatically if it has not been picked up after a few seconds.
- If the player speaks to the Treasure Hunter twice, then right after interacting with him for the second time they press the [YES] option from the first interaction, they will be charged but won't receive the tip. The player is still able to receive a tip if they pay the Treasure Hunter again.
- Spawning a treasure chest from a block that has different variants can make it turn into a different variant of the block. For example, getting a treasure chest in the Shepherd's hut can turn the birch planks into dark oak planks.
- If the player breaks a mushroom in the "small cave in the gorge," the chest will drop as an item, then the treasure inside will appear in the player's inventory.
## History

|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Treasure Hunter's dialogue overriding the option selection.|

# Udel
Aliases: Udel's Abiphone Keynote
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Udel]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Mage Faction Questline ([[Crimson Isle Main Questline]]) Purchase an Abiphone ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells [[Abiphones]].
Location: [[Scarleton Bank]]
Coordinates: -78.5 108 -787.5
Udel is an [[NPC]] located in [[Scarleton]] that sells [[Abiphones]].
## Udel's Abiphone Keynote
Udel's Abiphone Keynote is an event that happens on the dates of Apple Inc. events. During this event, new [[Abiphones|Abiphone]] models are announced and revealed.
Udel's Abiphone Keynote:
|Year|Name|Initial Cost|New Feature|
|-|-|-|-|
|2022|Abiphone XIV Orange ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]||
|2022|Abiphone XIV Black ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]||
|2022|Abiphone XIV Purple ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]||
|2023|Abiphone Flip: Dragon ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|Ringtones|
|2023|Abiphone Flip: Nucleus ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|Ringtones|
|2023|Abiphone Flip: Volcano ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|Ringtones|
|2025|Abiphone Flip+: Flower ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|Speed Dial|
|2025|Abiphone Flip+: Clockwork ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|Speed Dial|
|2025|Abiphone Flip+: Lunar ([[Abiphones]])|100,000,000 [[Coins]]|Speed Dial|
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; Objective: Find the Abiphone shop|[NPC] Udel: Oh, you must be [player]! My cousin told me you'd come by my place. [NPC] Udel: The friends of the Dean are my friends too! [NPC] Udel: I'll give you a 1.5% cost reduction on all the items in my shop!! [NPC] Udel: I have a wide range of Abiphones, from the Abiphone X+ to the Abiphone XIII Pro Giga!|
|Buying an Abiphone; Objective: Find the Abiphone shop|[NPC] Udel: Great! I'll let you know when I have a more expensive version of the same Abiphone available! [NPC] Udel: Sorry I mean a more powerful version of the Abiphone for the same price. [NPC] Udel: Anyways, Abiphones are magical devices that allow you to send sounds towards another Abiphone. [NPC] Udel: It might be hard for you to wrap your head around that concept, but you'll get used to it. Trust me. [NPC] Udel: Most people around here have an Abiphone, and some of them might let you add them to your contacts. [NPC] Udel: If you have someone's contact you can call them at any time, and they can also call you whenever. [NPC] Udel: I took the liberty to add Dean to your contacts already. I made sure you couldn't remove it, accidents happen! [NPC] Udel: Have a good day! Make sure you come back later to check my stock!|
|Udel's Abiphone Keynote Interaction|[NPC] Udel: Shh! [NPC] Udel: I'm in the middle of presenting the new Abiphone!|
|Udel's Abiphone Keynote Abiphone XIV ([[Abiphones]]) Announcement|[NPC] Udel: Hello fellow customer! [NPC] Udel: I am glad you could make it! [NPC] Udel: Today, I am unveiling the newest technology in Abiphones. [NPC] Udel: We are proud to present: [NPC] Udel: ABIPHONE XIV [NPC] Udel: Our latest addition to the Abiphone line has space for an extra contact! [NPC] Udel: And with the latest technology, you can add FIVE more music discs! [NPC] Udel: It would be better if they were playable... [NPC] Udel: Ahum. [NPC] Udel: Our new model comes in 3 atunning colors! [NPC] Udel: The Abiphone XIV is our best model yet. [NPC] Udel: And the price is still only 100m coins! [NPC] Udel: On top of that, if you own an old Abiphone XIII, you can trade it in! [NPC] Udel: The future of Abiphones is TODAY! [NPC] Udel: The Abiphone XIV is now for sale!|
|Udel's Abiphone Keynote Abiphone Flip ([[Abiphones]]) Announcement|[NPC] Udel: Hello customers! [NPC] Udel: I am so glad you could make it to my keynote! [NPC] Udel: In the past 72 years, my minions have worked hard to make a new Abiphone model. [NPC] Udel: I've obviously been working hard directing my minions! [NPC] Udel: I have made great technological leaps since our last model. [NPC] Udel: So without further ado, I am proud to present: [NPC] Udel: ... [NPC] Udel: Hello? [NPC] Udel: No, I'm in the middle of my keynote! [NPC] Udel: Yes, I'll call you back. [NPC] Udel: Ahem. [NPC] Udel: Sorry about that, I forgot to put my Abiphone on vibrate. [NPC] Udel: Anyways, I am proud to present: [NPC] Udel: ABIPHONE FLIP [NPC] Udel: This stunning new model has a brand-new speaker system. [NPC] Udel: Allowing you to choose a ringtone from our built-in selection! [NPC] Udel: Through sheer determination, I have also managed to find enough space for 1 extra contact! [NPC] Udel: To satisfy my shareholders, I had to double the price of this new Abiphone. [NPC] Udel: To satisfy my customers, I am giving everyone 50% off on their new Abiphone purchase! [NPC] Udel: This way, everyone is happy! [NPC] Udel: You can trade in your old Abiphone XIV for an even bigger discount! [NPC] Udel: The future of Abiphones is TODAY! [NPC] Udel: The Abiphone Flip is now for sale!|
|Udel's Abiphone Keynote Abiphone Flip+ ([[Abiphones]]) Announcement|[NPC] Udel: Hello customers! [NPC] Udel: I am so glad you could make it to my keynote! [NPC] Udel: First, I would like to apologize for this one being a little later than usual. [NPC] Udel: There is an ongoing lawsuit between myself and a counterfeit Abiphone ([[Abiphones & Co.]]), which has been taking up most of my time. [NPC] Udel: However, I am here, and so are you, so let's begin! [NPC] Udel: ... [NPC] Udel: Ahem. [NPC] Udel: Since my previous keynote, my minions and I have been even harder at work trying to find something even more innovative than the Abiphone Flip. [NPC] Udel: We thought and thought and then thought some more before I, and only I, had an incredible breakthrough. [NPC] Udel: What if our latest model could save you, like, a second, whenever you wanted to call someone? [NPC] Udel: After all, less downtime for my clientele means I get more uptime on my all-inclusive holidays! [NPC] Udel: So, without further ado, I am proud to present: [NPC] Udel: ABIPHONE FLIP [NPC] Udel: PLUS! [NPC] Udel: The most cutting edge technology around today, this new model comes with the Speed Dial feature! [NPC] Udel: This allows you to call your favorite contact with just a single click! [NPC] Udel: Also, in my infinite generosity, I have given this model an additional contact slot! [NPC] Udel: That's all for this year. Udel's Abiphone Services appreciate your continued support during the ongoing lawsuit! [NPC] Udel: So, without further ado... [NPC] Udel: The Abiphone Flip+ is now for sale!|
## Trivia
- Udel used to sell [[Abiphones]] for 1.5% less than their original prices.
- Udel is [[Dean]]'s cousin.
- According to [[Karis]], Udel created the Abiphone but forgot to patent it.

# Vanessa
Aliases: The Rainmaker
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Vanessa]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells rain for 5,000 [[Coins]] a minute.
Location: [[Birch Park]]
Coordinates: -304.5 76 -79.5
Vanessa is a shop [[NPC]] located in the [[Birch Park]]. She will make it rain for 5,000 [[Coins]] a minute.
## Mechanics
Vanessa can be paid 5,000 [[Coins]] to make the it rain in the [[Birch Park]] for 60s, with the limit of 30m.
While raining, players gain +25 [[Fishing Speed]]. Additionally, every 11 seconds there's a 66.6% (2 in 3 rolls) chance to spawn 1-2 [[Squid|Squids]] in water if there's less than 5 [[Squid|Squids]] alive.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Vanessa: I have the power to conjure rain from the skies. [NPC] Vanessa: But it'll cost you!|
## Trivia
- Hovering over The Rainmaker in the shop will tell you how much the SkyBlock community has spent on buying rain.
  - As of May 2026, this is about 178.9B [[Coins]].
- Rain costs 83.3 [[Coins]] per second and 300,000 [[Coins]] per hour.

|Harena Fortuna; Gain 𝑥 [[Mining Fortune]] when mining [[Red Sand]]. Gain|2 [[Mining Fortune]]|4 [[Mining Fortune]]|6 [[Mining Fortune]]|8 [[Mining Fortune]]|10 [[Mining Fortune]]|12 [[Mining Fortune]]|14 [[Mining Fortune]]|16 [[Mining Fortune]]|18 [[Mining Fortune]]|20 [[Mining Fortune]]|
|Harena Fortuna; Gain 𝑥 [[Mining Fortune]] when mining [[Red Sand]]. [[Crimson Essence]]|100|200|300|400|500|600|700|800|900|1,000|
|Harena Fortuna; Gain 𝑥 [[Mining Fortune]] when mining [[Red Sand]]. Cumulative Cost|100|300|600|1,000|1,500|2,100|2,800|3,600|4,500|5,500|
|Crimson Training; Increase your [[Combat Wisdom]] by 𝑥 while on the Crimson Isle and in the Kuudra Boss Fight. Increase|3 [[Combat Wisdom]]|5 [[Combat Wisdom]]|10 [[Combat Wisdom]]||||||||
|(same as above) [[Crimson Essence]]|250|1,250|5,000||||||||
|(same as above) Cumulative Cost|250|1,500|6,500||||||||

|(same as above) [[Crimson Essence]]|500|750|1,250|2,000|3,000||||||
|(same as above) Cumulative Cost|500|1,250|2,500|4,500|7,500||||||
## Trivia
- Vulcan is a reference to the Roman god of the same name. They were the God of Fire, Deserts, Forge and Volcanoes.
- Harena Fortuna is the Spanish for 'sand fortune', despite the perk only activating with [[Red Sand]] and not regular [[Sand]].

Walter is an [[NPC]] located at the back of the [[Gunpowder Mines]] in the [[Deep Caverns]]. They sell [[Superboom TNT]] for 2,500 [[Coins]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|None|[NPC] Walter: With the right tools, you can get through anything!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Walter: ✆ My abiphone is for Platinum-level donors of the Walter cause only. [NPC] Walter: ✆ You know these superbooms don't craft themselves right? [NPC] Walter: ✆ You just need Sulphur Collection 7 and to then donate an Enchanted Sulphur Cube! [GIVE ITEM]|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Walter: ✆ Welcome to the Platinum club, high roller! [NPC] Walter: ✆ Call me anytime! [NPC] Walter: ✆ And before you ask... yes, I do try to commercialize all of my friendships.|

- Walter's skin, originally titled Expert Demolitionist was created by DinowCookie on Planet Minecraft.

The Weaponsmith is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Combat Settlement]]. They sell starter [[Weapons]].
## Dialogue

# Windmill Operator
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Farming, Quest giver, Quests
[Windmill Operator]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the same items as the [[Farm Merchant]].
Location: [[Windmill]]
Coordinates: 102.5 82 -282.5
The Windmill Operator is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Windmill]]. They ask the player to bring them 64 (1 stack) [[String]] and 64 [[Cobblestone]]. After completing their quest, they unlock the [[Farm Merchant]]'s shop for the player.
## Dialogue
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Windmill Operator: Hello there, adventurer! [NPC] Windmill Operator: I work as the operator of this windmill. [NPC] Windmill Operator: However, I seem to have broken some key components in the structure. [NPC] Windmill Operator: Could you help me fix it? [NPC] Windmill Operator: I need a lot of string to fix the pulley system, could you bring me 64 of it?|
|Without [[String]]|[NPC] Windmill Operator: This isn't what I need! [NPC] Windmill Operator: I need a lot of string to fix the pulley system, could you bring me 64 of it?|
|Bringing 64 [[String]]|[NPC] Windmill Operator: I need some cobblestone to correct the base of the windmill, could you bring me 64 of it?|
|Without [[Cobblestone]]|[NPC] Windmill Operator: This isn't what I need! [NPC] Windmill Operator: I need some cobblestone to correct the base of the windmill, could you bring me 64 of it?|
|Bringing 64 [[Cobblestone]]|[NPC] Windmill Operator: Thank you! [NPC] Windmill Operator: Now that my windmill is up and running, I'll open my shop to you so you don't have to go back to the Village.|
## Trivia
- Windmill Operator shares a stock with [[Farm Merchant]], meaning the 640 daily buy limit cannot be bypassed by buying from both merchants.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 15, 2026|Added the [[Rookie Farming Axe]] and Beginner Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]) to the shop.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Added Windmill Operator.|
|Mar 24, 2023|Fixed various typos in the Windmill Operator's dialogue.|
|Jul 12, 2023|0.19.1|Fixed Windmill Operator shop name.|
|Jul 22, 2026|0.26.1|Added the [[Rookie Farming Axe]] and Beginner Agronomy Sack ([[Agronomy Sack]]) to the shop.|

# Wizard
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed
[Wizard]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
[Locations]
Description: Allows the player to access the [[Rift Dimension]] through the Wizard Portal ([[Wizard Tower]]).
Location: [[Wizard Tower]]
Coordinates: 48.5 119 99.5
Description: Appears here after closing [[The Breach]].
Location: [[Mountain]]
Coordinates: -5.5 202 65.5
The Wizard is an [[NPC]] who lives on the top floor of the [[Wizard Tower]]. They claim to be fixing [[Wizard Tower|The Wizard Portal]], and is replaced with the Wizard's Assistant after completing the [[Rift Dimension]], who functions identically.
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Below SkyBlock Level 12|[NPC] Wizard: Oh... [NPC] Wizard: I'm afraid crossing the portal would be a bit too dangerous for you. [NPC] Wizard: Learn more about this world before thinking of visiting other dimensions! (Requires SkyBlock Level 12)|
|First Interaction; SkyBlock Level 12 or above|[NPC] Wizard: Greetings, traveler! [NPC] Wizard: I can sense that you possess some magical prowess, and I find myself in need of assistance. [NPC] Wizard: The matter at hand is rather complex, but I shall be brief. [NPC] Wizard: Are you interested in learning more? Select an option: [Yes] [No]|
|Selecting [No]|[NPC] Wizard: Very well, I shall seek help elsewhere!|
|Selecting [Yes]|[NPC] Wizard: Excellent! [NPC] Wizard: You see, long ago a breach materialized atop the Mountain. [NPC] Wizard: Crossing through it is a daunting task, but not entirely impossible. [NPC] Wizard: The problem is... those who ventured through... never returned. [NPC] Wizard: I've dedicated much of my life to developing a portal that allows safe passage between our world and this mysterious dimension. Select an option: [And did you succeed?]|
|Selecting [And did you succeed?]|[NPC] Wizard: Indeed! [NPC] Wizard: With the invaluable help of my dear friend Barry, we managed to create a functioning portal. [NPC] Wizard: Barry tested it himself and successfully returned. [NPC] Wizard: He now uh... "studies" the Rift across. Select an option: [Why aren't you there yourself?]|
|Selecting [Why aren't you there yourself?]|[NPC] Wizard: Barry informed me that an alternate version of myself is trapped in that dimension, hailing from another world. [NPC] Wizard: According to the laws of causality, we must never meet, preventing me from crossing over. [NPC] Wizard: Imagine that! After devoting my life to this project, I cannot use it! [NPC] Wizard: Select an option: [Sounds bad. Why do you need me?]|
|Selecting [Sounds bad. Why do you need me?]|[NPC] Wizard: I have a reason to believe that a catastrophic event looms in our world's future. [NPC] Wizard: Unraveling the mysteries of the Rift could prove crucial to our survival. [NPC] Wizard: We need all the help we can muster, and you appear to be quite talented. Select an option: [Absolutely.] [I'm doing it for gear.]|
|Selecting [Absolutely.] or [I'm doing it for gear.]|[NPC] Wizard: Splendid. [NPC] Wizard: All you need is a Dimensional Infusion... [NPC] Wizard: ... then you can safely travel across. [NPC] Wizard: I can apply it to you every few hours, otherwise you will need to bring me enchanting orbs.|
|Jumping in the portal without; a Dimensional Infusion|[NPC] Wizard: Careful! [NPC] Wizard: You need a Dimensional Infusion before hopping in the portal. [NPC] Wizard: Come talk to me!|
|Jumping in the portal without; a Dimensional Infusion|[NPC] Wizard: Hold up there! [NPC] Wizard: You might explode in a million pieces if you go through the portal. [NPC] Wizard: Come talk to me first!|
|Jumping in the portal without; a Dimensional Infusion|[NPC] Wizard: If you want to safely travel through the portal, you need a Dimensional Infusion. [NPC] Wizard: Come talk to me!|
|Purchasing a Dimensional Infusion|[NPC] Wizard: Jump in the portal now!|
|Purchasing a Dimensional Infusion|[NPC] Wizard: You can jump into the portal now. [NPC] Wizard: Be careful, you might be surprised what's on the other side!|
|Purchasing a Dimensional Infusion|[NPC] Wizard: You should be able to jump into the portal now. [NPC] Wizard: Most people survive it!|
|After closing [[The Breach]] from [[The Mountaintop]]|[NPC] Wizard: Good lord. [NPC] Wizard: I came right as I heard the rumbling. [NPC] Wizard: Did you just... fix the breach? Select an option: [It was my destiny.] [NPC] Wizard: I cannot thank you enough. [NPC] Wizard: Here is a token of my gratitude for your actions.|
|After claiming [[There and Back Again]]|[NPC] Wizard: In addition to my other pursuits, I engage in novel writing during my leisure. [NPC] Wizard: I've composed a fictional narrative about an adventurer who ventures into the Rift and conquers it. [NPC] Wizard: While it's yet unpublished, I believe it would be fitting for you to have its cover. [NPC] Wizard: Now then, time is of the essence. [NPC] Wizard: I must study this new breach. [NPC] Wizard: Because of your help inside the Rift, once I am done, I will let you use my services for free. [NPC] Wizard: Please return in 3-5 business days!|
|At [[The Breach]] in the [[Mountain]]|[NPC] Wizard: Our knowledge of that era is minimal. [NPC] Wizard: But such an aura... [NPC] Wizard: I fear that even if I manage to open the breach, venturing through it would be a grave mistake. [NPC] Wizard: I can tell this is the Era of Kaeman, when he was at the zenith of his power.|
|At [[The Breach]] in the [[Mountain]]|[NPC] Wizard: I am now almost certain that the Breach leads to the Past. [NPC] Wizard: But to which point in time...? [NPC] Wizard: I must ascertain the answer!|
|At [[The Breach]] in the [[Mountain]]|[NPC] Wizard: I believe this breach was created by Jythar during the Calamity. [NPC] Wizard: However, I doubt it will permit us to travel that far back. [NPC] Wizard: I suspect someone is altering its destination.|
|At [[The Breach]] in the [[Mountain]]|[NPC] Wizard: I am making progress. [NPC] Wizard: I can now sense a great and formidable evil lurking beyond these threads of time.|
|At [[The Breach]] in the [[Mountain]]|[NPC] Wizard: It appears someone else is already utilizing the breach. [NPC] Wizard: I know not who they are, but their understanding exceeds mine!|
|During [[Spooky Festival]] [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]|[NPC] Wizard: There is certainly something quite...spooky...in the air!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Wizard: Where am I? Oh, it's you [player].|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Wizard: Thank you. This should be enough potatoes to generate about # volts. Might be just enough to cause a reaction.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Wizard: I suppose that was expected. Not many people have enough potatoes to power a magical portal.|
### Removed Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Jumping in the portal before [[Barry]] was elected in Year 189|[NPC] Wizard: Hmm...something appears to be broken. Give me 3-5 business days to fix!|
|Jumping in the portal after Barry ([[Candidate Barry]]) was elected in Year 189|[NPC] Wizard: Hmm...something appears to be broken. Give me 2-4 business days to fix!|
|Jumping in the portal after Barry ([[Candidate Barry]]) was elected in Year 191|[NPC] Wizard: Hmm...something appears to be broken. Give me 2-3 business days to fix!|
|Jumping in the portal after Barry ([[Candidate Barry]]) was elected in Year 221|[NPC] Wizard: Hmm...something appears to be broken. Give me 1-2 business days to fix!|
|Jumping in the portal after Barry ([[Candidate Barry]]) was elected in Year 223|[NPC] Wizard: Hmm...something appears to be broken. Give me 24 business hours to fix!|
|Jumping in the portal after Barry ([[Candidate Barry]]) retired|[NPC] Wizard: Hmmm... Something appears to be broken. Give me about 24 business hours to fix! Select an option: [It's been # days.] [NPC] Wizard: Oh. That's at least 4 business days. [NPC] Wizard: Time really flies! [NPC] Wizard: If only Barry had been helping instead of engaging in a popularity contest... [NPC] Wizard: Chasing clout? It's chasing clouds! [NPC] Wizard: Anyway, he seems to be hard at work now.|
|None; Before 0.19 Update|[NPC] Wizard: Check back later to use the Portal...it seems to be broken. :(|
|None; Before 0.19 Update|[NPC] Wizard: My Portal seems to be broken, [player]! [NPC] Wizard: Check back later...magic awaits!|
|None; Before 0.19 Update|[NPC] Wizard: The Portal hasn't worked since...I don't want to talk about it.|
|[[The Garden]]; Before 0.19 Update|[NPC] Wizard: Where am I? Oh, it's you [player]. [NPC] Wizard: Attempt number # of trying to fix the portal failed and I ended up here. Maybe it needs just a little more power...perhaps a battery made of potatoes?|
## Trivia
- The Wizard is referenced in [[Tal Ker]]'s dialogue ("The other day I met some kind of wizard and they told me to come back in 3 to 5 business days. That was over a year ago, still nothing. What kind of people do that? Tells you to come back in a few days for no reason. Really makes you think.")
- During Year 189, the name of the Wizard would sometimes show as obfuscated text of 6 letters.

# Zarina

Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs

Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Allows the player to upgrade the [[Midas' Sword]] and [[Midas Staff]].
Location: [[Trade Center]]
Coordinates: -32.5 70 -75.5
Zarina is an [[NPC]] in the [[Trade Center]] that allows the player to upgrade the [[Midas' Sword]] and [[Midas Staff]] by increasing the price paid for the items. This costs the price difference as well as 3 [[Stock of Stonks]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Zarina: Have you ever wanted to change fate? [NPC] Zarina: Unsay something you shouldn't've, undo a mistake you've made? [NPC] Zarina: Well, I can't help you do any of that, but I can increase the amount of Coins paid on Midas weapons so you can harness their maximum ❁ Damage bonus. [NPC] Zarina: And really, isn't that practically the next-best thing?|

# Zodd
Categories: Shops, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Zodd]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Description: Sells the [[General's Medallion]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]].
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Zodd is an [[NPC]] located in [[The Catacombs - Floor V]] and above. They sell the [[General's Medallion]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]
## Trivia
- The name Zodd could be a reference to Dru-Zod, better known as General Zod, a villain from DC Comics, being the leader of Krypton's military forces.
- Zodd's skin could be based on the appearance of Vandal Savage, an immortal villain from DC Comics, who served as an advisor and general to powerful leaders including Napoleon Bonaparte and Otto von Bismarck. Modern Era Vandal Savage has close ties to the government and war industry and is often depicted as having grey skin, a large gash over his left eye, and a large imperial pelisse.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 28, 2024|AHN 0.19.12|Added Zodd.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Mar 12, 2024|0.19.12|Added Zodd.|

# Zog
Categories: Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Pet items, 0.7.8

Zog is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Pet Care]]. They sell various [[Pet Items]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Zog: Hello! Have you heard of pet items? [NPC] Zog: Pet items give your companions bonuses! [NPC] Zog: I sell all kinds of them if you'd like to try one out!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Zog: ✆ You know [player], I'm a pet lover. [NPC] Zog: ✆ So my abiphone contacts list is FILLED with TONS of other pet lovers. [NPC] Zog: ✆ I don't want to sound too gamey but... [NPC] Zog: ✆ If you had 5 Max Lvl pets of Legendary tier or better in your pet menu... [NPC] Zog: ✆ That would show me you're a pet lover too!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) Meeting Requirements|[NPC] Zog: ✆ Wow, you're a true pet lover! [NPC] Zog: ✆ You doubled my HUGE contacts list too! [NPC] Zog: ✆ Now there's both Kat AND you!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Zog: I have a big problem. I lost my sense of taste, now I can't tell the difference between a melon and a cactus...|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Zog: Eating enough of these surely should bring back my taste memories. Thank you.|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Zog: My tongue is hurting for some reason...|

# Alchemy
Categories: Skills, Pages with UIs, Potions, Brewing, Alchemy
[Alchemy]
Type: Skill
[Properties]
Skill Max Level: 50
Alchemy is one of the [[Skills]] available for players to level up, primarily by brewing [[Potions]].
## Leveling XP
By brewing potions, players can increase their Alchemy level as outlined in the table below. For more information on how to brew specific potions please visit the [[Potions]] page.
|Item|XP Yield|Area|
|-|-|-|
|Nether Wart|+1|[[Crimson Isle]]|
|Awkward Potion|+5|[[Private Island]]|
Note that Brewing experience is collected when the player takes the Potion out of the [[Brewing Stand]]. Potion leveling XP seems to be tied only to the first ingredient added to the awkward potion in most or all cases below. The ingredient column indicates which ingredient leads to this XP yield when added to the lowest level indicated in the level column.
Note: Adding [[Glowstone Dust]], [[Enchanted Glowstone Dust]], or [[Enchanted Glowstone]] does not increase XP rewards from brewing a potion.
|Potion|Ingredient|Added To|Xp Yield|Total Shop Sell Price (per 3 potions made)|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Speed ([[Speed Potion]])|[[Sugar]]|Awkward Potion|+5 Alchemy XP|I: 6 [[Coins]]; II: 9 [[Coins]]|
|Speed ([[Speed Potion]])|[[Enchanted Sugar]]|Awkward Potion|+300 Alchemy XP|III: 117 [[Coins]]; IV: 120 [[Coins]]|
|Speed ([[Speed Potion]])|[[Enchanted Sugar Cane]]|Awkward Potion|+15,000 Alchemy XP|V: 501 [[Coins]]; VI: 17,499 [[Coins]]; VII: 17,799 [[Coins]]; VIII: 86,001 [[Coins]]|
|Jump Boost ([[Jump Boost Potion]])|[[Rabbit's Foot]]|Awkward Potion|+12 Alchemy XP|I: 6 [[Coins]]; II: 9 [[Coins]]|

# Carpentry
Categories: Skills, Pages with UIs, Potions, Carpentry, Crafting, Cosmetics
[Carpentry]
Type: Skill
[Properties]
Skill Max Level: 50
Carpentry is one of the [[Skills]] that players can level up by [[Crafting]] items. It allows the player to craft and use Carpentry items using the [[Carpentry Table]].
The skill is unlocked after giving the [[Carpenter]] 64 (1 stack) [[Wool]], who will then reward the player with the recipe for the [[Carpentry Table]].
At Carpentry III (3), the player unlocks [[Crafting|Quick Crafting]], which allows them to instantly craft an item, as long as they have the required materials in their inventory. However, it is only available to players with the 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] rank and above. On [[Bingo|Ⓑ Bingo]] profiles, it is available to all players, including those without a rank. On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profiles, it is only available to 3[[Ranks|[VIP+]]] players and above.
## Furniture
Carpentry is the main way to obtain [[Furniture]], which can be crafted using the [[Carpentry Table]].
## Leveling Up
The player can level up Carpentry by crafting items. The XP gained is 3% of the combined NPC sell price of the ingredients used to craft the item. Most vanilla recipes do not grant Carpentry XP.

## Tips
- Crafting [[Power Scrolls|Sapphire Power Scrolls]] can be a good way of leveling Carpentry. It gives a decent amount of Carpentry XP while profiting from selling the Power Scrolls on the Auction House.
- For a Bazaar-Only approach, forum user Prekoyte has developed an online tool to calculate the most efficient crafts for Carpentry XP.
## Trivia
- Upgrading to Carpentry IV (4) or above used to require 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] before SkyBlock 0.14.
- Carpentry used to have an XP multiplier that differed between [[Ranks]]. It was subsequently removed in SkyBlock 0.14.
- Crafting items in the vanilla 2x2 crafting grid in the player's inventory does not give Carpentry XP, even if it normally does when crafted in the 3x3 crafting grid.
- A player gets experience proportional to how many items are required. It's most efficient to get items which tier up such as [[Perfect Armor]], [[Griffin Upgrade Stone|Griffin Upgrade Stones]], etc.

# Combat
Aliases: Combat Exp, Combat Xp, Combat exp, Combat xp
Categories: Skills, Pages with UIs, Potions, Combat
[Combat]
Type: Skill
[Properties]
Skill Max Level: 60
Combat is one of the core player [[Skills]] in SkyBlock. It can be leveled up by killing mobs, claiming Combat [[Minions|Minion]] drops, claiming [[Bestiary]] milestones, and a multitude of other methods. The maximum level Combat can go up to is Combat LX (60).
## Overview
Combat XP is gained from killing mobs: each mob grants a set amount of XP when killed, with the exception of mobs spawned on the [[Private Island]], which do not grant XP on kill.
The amount of Combat XP gained on mob kill increases with [[Combat Wisdom]]. [[Combat Wisdom]] can be increased through numerous different methods, such as using different weapons and armor that grant [[Combat Wisdom]], equipping [[Pets]] that grant [[Combat Wisdom]], or syphoning the [[Apex Dragon Shard]].
## Leveling Rewards
The cumulative rewards for leveling up Combat are as follows:

## Warrior Bonus
By leveling up your Combat skill, you gain a passive Warrior bonus. For every level from Combat I (1) to Combat L (50), all [[Damage]] against mobs is increased by 4%: Combat LI (51) to Combat LX (60) grants +1% [[Damage]] instead.

- [[Bestiary]]

# Enchanting
Categories: Skills, Pages with UIs, Potions, Pages with math equations, Dungeons
[Enchanting]
Type: Skill
[Properties]
Skill Max Level: 60
Enchanting is one of the [[Skills]] available for players to level up as they perform certain actions that give them Enchanting XP.
[[Roman Numerals]] are used to express its level in-game.
## Leveling XP
Enchanting items in an [[Enchantment Table]] or Anvil grants Enchanting XP relative to the number of levels spent. The amount of Enchanting XP gained from spending X levels is given by XP=3.5X32.
It is also possible to gain Enchanting XP from fishing up [[Grand Experience Bottle|Grand Experience Bottles]] or [[Titanic Experience Bottle|Titanic Experience Bottles]].
Additionally, the [[Experimentation Table]] can be used to gain significant amounts of Enchanting XP.

## Conjurer
Conjurer is an ability exclusively unlocked by leveling up the Enchanting Skill. Each level of Conjurer grants 5% more [[Experience]] from any source. This also makes Enchanting the only skill to offer a 5% bonus in its respective stat per level, as opposed to the 4% bonus per level of most other skills.
Each level of Enchanting grants one level of Conjurer. For example, if the player were on Enchanting level 15, the player would be on Conjurer level 15.

## List of Enchantments
A link to a list of all enchantments can be found [[Enchantments|here]].
## Trivia
- Although Enchanting goes from levels 1-60, there is a 50 XP requirement to reach the first level, meaning there is a "level 0", just like all other [[Skills]].
- Enchanting items on the [[Enchantment Table]] may yield only a maximum of 500,000 Enchanting XP per day. The [[Experimentation Table]] is an alternative method of gaining Enchanting XP that bypasses the manual Enchanting XP limit of using an Enchantment Table or Anvil.
  - There is also a limit for Enchanting XP gained for the [[Experimentation Table]], but the limit is unknown.
- The [[Experimentation Table]] has a daily cooldown, but it is the most efficient way to earn Enchanting XP. Players should make the Experimentation Table their priority if wishing to maximize XP gains beyond Level 10.
- It costs nothing to combine [[Enchanted Book|Enchanted Books]] in an [[Anvil]].
- It also costs nothing to upgrade an existing Enchantment on an item with another Enchanted Book in an Anvil.
- The enchantment with the highest enchanting requirements is [[One For All]].

# Fishing
Aliases: Fishing Exp, Fishing Level, Fishing Skill, Fishing Xp, Fishing exp, Fishing level, Fishing skill, Fishing xp, Great Catch, Lava Fishing, Mountinside Pond
Categories: Skills, More info needed, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Fishing, Potions, Mechanics
[Fishing]
Type: Skill
[Properties]
Skill Max Level: 50
Fishing is one of the core player [[Skills]] in SkyBlock. Mainly, it can be leveled up by catching items such as [[Treasure]] or [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] or slaying [[Sea Creatures]]. Players can fish in either [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] or [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]], each with unique pools, but requiring a different [[Fishing Rods|Fishing Rod]].
When Fishing, players' catch is determined by several rolls. In order they are:
1. [[Sea Creatures]], chance of which is equal to their [[Sea Creature Chance]]. Sea Creatures have a numerical value called "[[Sea Creatures|Weight]]", which determines the odds of each Sea Creature and can be increased by various sources.
2. [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]], chance of which varies depending on Trophy and is increased by [[Trophy Chance]]. Specific Trophy and their tier is rolled from the rarest to the most common.
3. [[Treasure]], chance of which is equal to their [[Treasure Chance]]. [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Details about which determines the tier of treasure and which specific treasure is caught.; When this info is added, remove this.)
## Fishing
### Fishing Speed
[[Fishing Speed]] decreases the amount of time it takes the player to catch an item or Sea Creature.
Fishing Speed Cap defaults to 300, but varies on some islands.
|Island|Fishing Speed Cap|
|-|-|
|[[Backwater Bayou]]|200 [[Fishing Speed]]|
|[[Lotus Atoll]]|250 [[Fishing Speed]]|
|[[Crimson Isle]]|350 [[Fishing Speed]]|
|Everywhere else|300 [[Fishing Speed]]|
### Sea Creatures
When fishing, players have a chance to catch a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]]. Sea Creatures are mobs which drop Fishing loot and Fishing XP when killed. The player's [[Sea Creature Chance]] determines the chance they have to catch a Sea Creature and [[Double Hook Chance]] determines the chance of fishing two Sea Creatures at once.
As a player increases their fishing level, they unlock stronger creatures that give more XP and better loot. Killing sea creatures on the private island does not provide fishing XP.
Sea Creatures cannot be damaged with arrows directly, except those shot from [[Prismarine Bow]].
[[Runecrafting|Runic]] sea creatures have a 50% chance to drop a Wake Rune upon death.
Players can disable catching Sea Creatures by wearing at least 2 pieces of [[Bronze Hunter Armor]] or of any of its upgrades, or by using [[Treasure Hook]].
### Trophy Fishing
[[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] can be occasionally caught while fishing on the [[Lotus Atoll]] and [[Crimson Isle]]. [[Trophy Chance]] increases chances to catch [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]].
There are multiple tiers of each [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophy]] Fish: Bronze, Silver, Gold, and Diamond. [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] can exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] or [[Odger]] for [[Lotus]] or [[Magmafish]].
Catching individual tiers of [[Trophy (disambiguation)|Trophies]] increases the player's Trophy Fishing level, granting them rewards from [[Researcher Ribery]] and [[Odger]]. Additionally, reaching [[Odger|Novice]] and [[Odger|Adept Trophy Fisher]] unlocks the [[Thunder]] and [[Lord Jawbus]] [[Sea Creatures]].
Players can disable catching Trophies by wearing [[Tera Shell Necklace]], or by using [[Treasure Hook]].
### Treasure Fishing
Items can be obtained from fishing if the player does not catch a Sea Creature, depending on their [[Treasure Chance]].
There is a base chance of 89% for a [[Treasure|GOOD CATCH]], 10% for a [[Treasure|GREAT CATCH]], and 1% for an [[Treasure|OUTSTANDING CATCH]]. The chance for great and outstanding catches can be increased using [[Blessed Bait]], the [[Blessing (Enchantment)|Blessing]] enchantment, and a [[Rarity|RARE]] or better [[Hermit Crab Pet]].
### Fishing Rods
[[Fishing Rods]] are the main tools that player uses to fish. First one can be obtained from [[Fisherwoman Enid]], crafted or bought from [[Fishing Merchant]].
### Rod Parts
Rod Parts come in three variants: Hooks, Lines, and Sinkers. Hooks affect the chance to catch certain items or Sea Creatures, Lines grant global stat bonuses, and Sinkers grant Fishing Rods miscellaneous special abilities.
### Fishing Baits
Baits provide a variety of bonus effects to Fishing. They're consumed when catching something. If the player pulls their rod without catching anything, bait is not consumed. Players can reduce the chance for Bait to be consumed. Using bait is automatic, but can be disabled in the player's [[Bait Sack]].
### Hotspots
Once the they're introduced to the concept by [[Hattie]], players will be able to find [[Fishing Hotspots|Hotspots]] on most islands that allow fishing, in which player can fish unique [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] and gain minor stat boost.

## Tips
- There are two main reasons to fish, for the XP and for valuable drops. No matter which is being gone for, it is required to have as much [[Fishing Speed]] as possible. When fishing for XP, getting as much [[Fishing Wisdom]] as possible is recommended. When fishing for valuable drops (from Sea Creatures), getting as much [[Sea Creature Chance]] and [[Magic Find]] as possible is recommended. If possible, use the [[Whale Bait]], which is currently the best bait. If it is too expensive, [[Glowy Chum Bait]] or [[Fish Bait]] serve well as a cheaper alternative.
## Notes
- It is impossible to fish in [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] or [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] inside [[Dungeons]], but it is still possible to use [[Dirt Rod]].[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; when was water/lava fishing disabled in Dungeons; When this info is added, remove this.)
## Trivia
- There are currently 5 [[Fairy Souls]] that can only be obtained by fishing. Two of them can only be fished up in the [[Fairy Grotto]].
- The [[Fishing Crystal]] that spawns with the pond island grants more frequent drops of [[Prismarine Shard|Prismarine Shards]] and [[Prismarine Crystals]].
- Daytime occurs between 6:00 am–7:00 pm whilst Night time occurs between 7:00 pm–6:00 a.m. It is also indicated by the sun or moon next to the time in the sidebar.
- The Combat XP buffs from [[Slayer]], [[Potions|Combat XP Boost Potion]], [[Viking's Tear]], or [[Accessories]] like [[Hunter Ring]] apply to Fishing XP earned from killing sea creatures. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- XP from Sea Creatures based on Zombies ([[Sea Walker]], [[Rider of the Deep|Monster of the Deep]], [[Water Hydra]]) count for [[Zombie Slayer]]. Similarly, [[Lava Flame|Lava Flames]], the only Blaze Sea Creature, count for [[Blaze Slayer]].
- The chicken of the [[Rider of the Deep|Monster of the Deep]] drops more XP. But for a Runic [[Rider of the Deep|Monster of the Deep]] only killing the Zombie yields a rune. [Confirm]
- Spooky fishing mobs can be caught whenever the [[Fear Mongerer]] is in the hub, not just during the spooky festival.
